<script id="model-ui">
'use strict';
(()=>{
  const M=globalThis.RaceModelCore,$=id=>document.getElementById(id);let dataset=null,report=null,busy=false;
  const el=(tag,value,cls)=>{const n=document.createElement(tag);if(value!==undefined)n.textContent=String(value);if(cls)n.className=cls;return n;};
  const yen=v=>v===null||v===undefined?'—':v.toLocaleString('ja-JP',{maximumFractionDigits:2})+'円',decimal=v=>v===null||v===undefined?'—':v.toLocaleString('ja-JP',{maximumFractionDigits:6}),percent=v=>v===null||v===undefined?'—':(v*100).toLocaleString('ja-JP',{maximumFractionDigits:4})+'%',loss=(v,inf)=>inf?'∞':decimal(v),sport=s=>s==='horse'?'競馬':'競輪';
  const status={PURCHASED:'購入済み（架空）',HYPOTHETICAL:'仮想購入',NOT_PURCHASED:'未購入'};
  function setError(s){$('modelError').textContent=s;}function message(s){$('modelStatus').textContent=s;}
  $('showModel').addEventListener('click',()=>{for(const id of ['phase1View','historyView','qualityView'])$(id).hidden=true;for(const id of ['showEV','showHistory','showQuality'])$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('modelView').hidden=false;$('showModel').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('modelHeading').focus();});
  for(const id of ['showEV','showHistory','showQuality'])$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{$('modelView').hidden=true;$('showModel').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});
  function countOptions(){const s=$('modelInputSport').value,old=$('modelInputCount').value,nums=s==='horse'?Array.from({length:17},(_,i)=>i+2):[7,9];$('modelInputCount').replaceChildren();for(const n of nums){const o=el('option',n+(s==='horse'?'頭':'車'));o.value=n;$('modelInputCount').append(o);}$('modelInputCount').value=nums.includes(+old)?old:s==='horse'?'4':'7';}
  $('modelInputSport').addEventListener('change',countOptions);countOptions();
  function metrics(values){const dl=el('dl',undefined,'model-metrics');for(const [name,value] of values){const box=el('div',undefined,'metric');box.append(el('dt',name),el('dd',value));dl.append(box);}return dl;}
  function table(headers,rows,cls='model-table'){const wrap=el('div',undefined,'table-scroll');wrap.tabIndex=0;wrap.setAttribute('role','region');wrap.setAttribute('aria-label',headers.join('・'));const t=el('table',undefined,cls),head=el('thead'),tr=el('tr'),body=el('tbody');for(const h of headers)tr.append(el('th',h));head.append(tr);for(const values of rows){const row=el('tr');for(const v of values)row.append(el('td',v));body.append(row);}t.append(head,body);wrap.append(t);return wrap;}
  function selectedPrediction(){return dataset?.predictions.find(p=>p.id===$('modelRace').value)??null;}
  function prediction(){const p=selectedPrediction(),body=$('modelDistributionRows'),warnings=$('modelPredictionWarnings'),details=$('modelFeatureDetails');body.replaceChildren();warnings.replaceChildren();details.replaceChildren();$('modelPredictionExport').disabled=busy||!p;
    $('modelPredictionInfo').textContent=p?'SYNTHETIC DEMO / '+sport(p.sport)+' '+p.raceId+' / '+p.model.kind+' '+p.model.version+' / 予測 '+p.predictionAt+' / 記録 '+p.recordedAt:'保存された予測はありません。';$('modelDistributionSum').textContent=p?'全'+p.classes.length+'クラス / 合計 '+percent(p.probabilities.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0))+'（未丸めで総和検証PASS）':'';
    if(!p)return;for(let i=0;i<p.classes.length;i++){const tr=el('tr');tr.append(el('td',p.betType),el('td',p.classes[i]),el('td',percent(p.probabilities[i])));body.append(tr);}for(const w of p.warnings)warnings.append(el('li',w));const dl=el('dl');for(const [name,value] of [['事前特徴量SHA-256',p.featureHash],['予測SHA-256',p.snapshotHash],['予測ID',p.id],['モデル出所',p.model.source],['学習レース',JSON.stringify(p.model.trainedRaceKeys)],['学習終了時刻',p.model.trainedUntil??'未学習'],['監査参照（入力へコピーしません）',p.featureSnapshot.auditRef??'なし']])dl.append(el('dt',name),el('dd',value));details.append(dl);
    for(const f of p.featureSnapshot.features){const card=el('div',undefined,'model-card');card.append(el('h4',f.name),el('p',JSON.stringify(f.value)),el('p','出所: '+f.source,'sources'),el('p','公表 '+f.publishedAt+' / 取得 '+f.acquiredAt+' / 使用可能 '+f.availableAt,'sources'),el('p','欠損状態: '+String(f.missing),'sources'));details.append(card);}
  }
  function render(){
    const old=$('modelRace').value;$('modelRace').replaceChildren();for(const p of dataset?.predictions??[]){const o=el('option',sport(p.sport)+' / '+p.raceId+' / '+p.classes.length+'クラス');o.value=p.id;$('modelRace').append(o);}if(!dataset?.predictions.length){const o=el('option','記録なし');o.value='';$('modelRace').append(o);}if(dataset?.predictions.some(p=>p.id===old))$('modelRace').value=old;prediction();
    report=dataset?M.evaluate(dataset,$('modelRole').value):null;const filter=$('modelSport').value,scope=r=>!filter||r.sport===filter;
    $('modelSplitInfo').textContent=dataset?'レース単位の割当: train '+dataset.splits.train.length+' / validation '+dataset.splits.validation.length+' / test '+dataset.splits.test.length+'。時系列・ラベル利用時刻の分離検査PASS（申告値に対する検査）。':'学習・評価データなし。実学習は行いません。';
    const selected=report?.rows.filter(scope)??[];$('modelEvaluationCounts').textContent='SYNTHETIC DEMO / '+$('modelRole').value+' / 表示対象 '+selected.length+'レース・評価 '+selected.filter(r=>r.evaluated).length+'・未評価 '+selected.filter(r=>!r.evaluated).length;
    $('modelMissing').textContent=report?'全競技：予測記録欠損 '+report.missingPredictions+'レース / 結果・ラベル欠損 '+report.missingResults+'レース / 予測内の安定ID欠損 '+report.missingStableIds+'件。個体履歴の学習準備は未完了です。':'';
    const groups=$('modelGroupResults');groups.replaceChildren();for(const g of report?.groups.filter(scope)??[]){const card=el('div',undefined,'model-card');card.dataset.modelGroup=g.sport;card.append(el('h4','SYNTHETIC DEMO / '+sport(g.sport)+' '+g.betType),el('p','比較対象 '+g.evaluated+'レース / 未評価 '+g.unevaluated+'レース','sources'),metrics([['Brier',decimal(g.brier)],['Log Loss',loss(g.logLoss,g.logLossInfinite)],['均等基準Brier',decimal(g.baselineBrier)],['Brier差（候補−基準）',decimal(g.brierDifference)],['Log Loss差（候補−基準）',loss(g.logLossDifference,g.logLossInfinite)],['ECE / 全クラス対',decimal(g.calibration.ece)+' / '+g.calibration.count+'件']]));const cal=el('details');cal.append(el('summary','架空の校正表（10ビン）'),table(['確率区間','クラス対件数','平均予測確率','ラベル率'],g.calibration.bins.map((b,i)=>[percent(b.from)+'〜'+percent(b.to)+(i===9?'（上端含む）':'（上端未満）'),b.count,percent(b.meanProbability),percent(b.observedRate)])));card.append(cal);groups.append(card);}
    const evalRows=$('modelEvaluationRows');evalRows.replaceChildren();for(const r of selected){const tr=el('tr');for(const v of [sport(r.sport)+' / '+r.raceId,r.betType,r.evaluated?'評価 / '+r.label:'未評価',decimal(r.score?.brier),loss(r.score?.logLoss,r.score?.logLossInfinite),r.reason??'—'])tr.append(el('td',v));evalRows.append(tr);}
    const back=$('modelBacktest');back.replaceChildren();for(const st of ['PURCHASED','HYPOTHETICAL','NOT_PURCHASED']){const all=report?.ticketRows.filter(r=>scope(r)&&r.purchaseStatus===st)??[],valid=all.filter(r=>r.evaluated),sum=k=>valid.reduce((a,r)=>a+r[k],0),invest=sum('investment'),profit=sum('profit'),forecast=sum('forecastProfit'),card=el('div',undefined,'model-card');card.dataset.purchaseStatus=st;card.append(el('h4','SYNTHETIC DEMO / '+status[st]),el('p',all.length+'件 / 評価 '+valid.length+'件 / 未評価 '+(all.length-valid.length)+'件','sources'));
      if(st==='NOT_PURCHASED')card.append(el('p','未購入のため、投資・払戻・実現損益へ算入しません。'));else card.append(metrics([['評価済み投資',valid.length?yen(invest):'—'],['予測期待損益',valid.length?yen(forecast):'—'],['実現損益',valid.length?yen(profit):'—'],['予測期待回収率',invest?percent((forecast+invest)/invest):'—'],['実現回収率',invest?percent(sum('payout')/invest):'—'],['実現−予測期待損益',valid.length?yen(profit-forecast):'—']]));back.append(card);}
    const ticketRows=$('modelTicketRows');ticketRows.replaceChildren();for(const r of report?.ticketRows.filter(scope)??[]){const tr=el('tr');for(const v of [r.id+' / '+r.raceId+' / '+r.combination,status[r.purchaseStatus],percent(r.grossEV),yen(r.forecastProfit),yen(r.profit),r.evaluated?(r.hit?'的中':'不的中'):r.reason])tr.append(el('td',v));ticketRows.append(tr);}
    for(const id of ['modelExport','modelReport'])$(id).disabled=busy||!dataset;for(const id of ['modelDemo','modelFile','modelCreate','modelFeatureExample','modelFeatureText','modelInputSport','modelInputCount'])$(id).disabled=busy;
  }
  function action(fn){try{setError('');fn();}catch(e){setError('処理できません。既存モデルデータを保持しました。'+e.message);}render();}
  $('modelDemo').addEventListener('click',()=>action(()=>{const next=M.demoDataset();M.validateDataset(next);dataset=next;message('SYNTHETIC DEMO：固定架空例 '+dataset.races.length+'レース・予測 '+dataset.predictions.length+'件を読込。実予測/実戦成績ではありません。');}));
  $('modelFeatureExample').addEventListener('click',()=>action(()=>{const now=new Date().toISOString(),s=$('modelInputSport').value,n=+$('modelInputCount').value,f=M.demoFeature(s,n,'SYNTHETIC-INPUT-'+s+'-'+now,now.slice(0,10));f.predictionAt=now;f.startAt=new Date(Date.parse(now)+3600000).toISOString();for(const v of f.features){v.publishedAt=now;v.acquiredAt=now;v.availableAt=now;}$('modelFeatureText').value=JSON.stringify(f,null,2);message('SYNTHETIC DEMOの事前入力例を表示。開始は端末時計の1時間後、安定IDは欠損です。');}));
  $('modelCreate').addEventListener('click',()=>action(()=>{
    const raw=$('modelFeatureText').value;globalThis.RaceQualityCore.utf8(raw,M.MAX_FEATURE);const f=JSON.parse(raw),p=M.makePrediction(f,new Date().toISOString()),next=dataset?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(dataset)):{format:'RACELAB_MODEL_DEMO_V1',dataKind:'SYNTHETIC',races:[],predictions:[],results:[],tickets:[],splits:{train:[],validation:[],test:[]}};if(next.races.some(r=>M.raceKey(r)===M.raceKey(p)))throw Error('同じレースの予測/レースを上書きできません。');next.races.push({sport:p.sport,raceId:p.raceId,dataKind:p.dataKind,startAt:p.startAt});next.predictions.push(p);next.splits.test.push(M.raceKey(p));M.validateDataset(next);dataset=next;render();$('modelRace').value=p.id;prediction();message('SYNTHETIC DEMO：事前予測1件を記録。結果を作らず未評価で保持しました。');
  }));
  $('modelFile').addEventListener('change',async()=>{if(busy)return;busy=true;setError('');render();try{const f=$('modelFile').files[0];if(!f||f.size>M.MAX_FILE)throw Error('モデルJSONは4MiBまでです。');const raw=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer()),next=M.decode(raw);dataset=next;message('SYNTHETIC DEMO：ハッシュ・分布・区間の照合PASS。'+dataset.races.length+'レースを読込。');}catch(e){setError('追加/置換0件。既存モデルデータを保持しました。'+e.message);}finally{busy=false;render();}});
  $('modelRace').addEventListener('change',prediction);for(const id of ['modelRole','modelSport'])$(id).addEventListener('change',()=>action(()=>{}));
  function save(text,name){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'})),a=el('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;document.body.append(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
  $('modelExport').addEventListener('click',()=>action(()=>save(M.encode(dataset),'SYNTHETIC_model_dataset.json')));
  $('modelReport').addEventListener('click',()=>action(()=>save(JSON.stringify({format:'RACELAB_EVALUATION_DEMO_V1',disclaimer:'SYNTHETIC DEMO：実予測精度・実戦回収率ではありません。',report:M.evaluate(dataset,$('modelRole').value)},null,2),'SYNTHETIC_evaluation_report.json')));
  $('modelPredictionExport').addEventListener('click',()=>action(()=>{const p=selectedPrediction();M.verifyPrediction(p);save(JSON.stringify(p,null,2),'SYNTHETIC_prediction.json');}));
  render();
})();

<script id="ability-ui">
'use strict';
(()=>{
 const U=globalThis.RaceUniCore,A=globalThis.RaceAbilityCore,$=id=>document.getElementById(id);let book=U.empty(),stage=null,editTarget=null,selected=null,revision=0;
 const key=r=>r.raceCard.sport+'|'+r.raceCard.raceId,el=(tag,text,cls)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;if(cls)e.className=cls;return e;};
 const current=()=>book.races.find(r=>key(r)===$('abilityRace').value);
 const conditionText=c=>Object.entries(c.conditions).map(([k,v])=>({distanceMetres:'距離(m)',surface:'馬場',going:'馬場状態',bankLengthMetres:'バンク長(m)',raceClass:'クラス'}[k]||k)+': '+(v===null?'未確認':v)).join(' / ');
 function status(msg,error=false){$('abilityError').textContent=error?msg:'';$('abilityStatus').textContent=error?'処理できませんでした。登録済みデータは保持されています。':msg;}
 function invalidate(){stage=null;revision++;$('abilityCommit').disabled=true;$('abilityPreview').hidden=true;}
 function dl(values){const d=el('dl');for(const[k,v]of values)d.append(el('dt',k),el('dd',v===null||v===''?'未確認 / 欠損':String(v)));return d;}
 function renderLines(r){const box=$('abilityLines');box.replaceChildren();const c=r.raceCard;if(c.sport!=='keirin')return;const l=c.lines;if(!l){box.append(el('p','ライン構成：未確認','warn'));return;}box.append(el('p','レース単位の'+(l.status==='CONFIRMED'?'確認済み構成（申告）':'想定構成（未確認）')+' · 個人能力への加点なし','ability-line-meta'));l.groups.forEach((g,i)=>{const x=el('div',undefined,'ability-line');x.append(el('strong','ライン'+(i+1)+'　'),el('span',g.join(' → ')));box.append(x);});box.append(el('p','出所: '+(l.source||'未確認')+' / 確認: '+(l.checkedAt||'未確認'),'ability-line-meta'));}
 function renderDetail(r,n){const row=U.ranked(r).find(x=>x.number===n);$('abilityCards').replaceChildren();if(!row)return;const v=row.evaluation,a=row.assessment,card=el('article',undefined,'ability-card');card.dataset.number=n;card.append(el('h3',n+'番 '+(row.name||'名前欠損')),el('p',(row.rank===null?'順位未評価':'仮順位 '+row.rank+'位')+' / '+(v.score===null?'未評価':v.score+'点')+' / 根拠率 '+(a.coverage===null?'未算出':a.coverage+'%'),'ability-score'));
  if(row.edited)card.append(el('p','USER手修正あり。採点式の参考点: '+(a.score===null?'未算出':a.score+'点')+'。原評価・根拠は下に保持。','warn'));
  card.append(el('p',a.kind==='STRUCTURED'?'評価済 '+a.assessed+'/6項目 / 不足: '+(a.missing.join('、')||'なし'):'旧/独自ルール。根拠率は算出していません。','warn'));
  if(r.raceCard.sport==='keirin')card.append(el('p','ライン内の位置: '+U.position(r,n)));
  if(row.edited||a.kind==='LEGACY')card.append(el('p',(row.edited?'修正後の理由: ':'評価理由: ')+(v.reason||'未確認')));
  card.append(el('p','原評価の出所: '+(v.source||'未確認')+' / 確認時刻: '+(v.checkedAt||'未確認'),'sources'));
  const details=el('details');details.open=true;details.append(el('summary','項目別：事実・AI評価・未確認'));
  if(a.kind==='STRUCTURED')for(const b of a.items){const s=el('details',undefined,'ability-evidence-item');s.dataset.item=b.id;s.open=b.id==='recentPerformance';s.append(el('summary',b.label+' / 重み'+b.weight+'% / '+(b.score===null?'未評価':b.score+'点'),b.score===null?'missing':''),dl([['確認事実',b.事実],['AI評価',b.AI評価],['未確認',b.未確認],['出所',b.出所],['確認時刻',b.確認時刻]]));details.append(s);}else details.append(el('p',r.evaluations.find(e=>e.number===n).reason||'未確認'));
  const facts=el('details');facts.append(el('summary','元の出走表記載情報・安定ID'),dl([['安定ID',row.stableId??'欠損（番号で代用しません）'],...Object.entries(row.facts)]));
  const context=el('details');context.open=r.raceCard.sport==='keirin';context.append(el('summary','条件・ラインのAI所見（個人能力と別）'),el('p',v.contextNote||'未確認'));
  const history=el('details');history.append(el('summary','AI原評価と手修正履歴'));const original=r.evaluations.find(e=>e.number===n);history.append(el('p','原評価点: '+(original.score===null?'未評価':original.score+'点')));const edits=r.edits.filter(e=>e.number===n);for(const t of edits)history.append(el('p','USER '+t.editedAt+' / 採点版 '+t.ruleId+' v'+t.ruleVersion),el('p','修正前: '+JSON.stringify(t.before)),el('p','修正後: '+JSON.stringify(t.after)));if(!edits.length)history.append(el('p','手修正なし'));
  const button=el('button','点数・理由を手修正');button.dataset.edit=n;button.addEventListener('click',()=>openEditor(key(r),n));card.append(button,context,details,facts,history);$('abilityCards').append(card);
 }
 function render(k=$('abilityRace').value){const list=book.races.filter(r=>$('abilitySport').value==='all'||r.raceCard.sport===$('abilitySport').value);$('abilityRace').replaceChildren();for(const r of list){const o=el('option',(r.raceCard.sport==='horse'?'競馬':'競輪')+' / '+r.raceCard.raceId);o.value=key(r);$('abilityRace').append(o);}if(list.some(r=>key(r)===k))$('abilityRace').value=k;if(!list.length){const o=el('option','登録なし');o.value='';$('abilityRace').append(o);}
  $('abilityCount').textContent=book.races.length+'レース / '+book.races.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.raceCard.expectedCount,0)+'名';for(const id of ['abilitySaveJSON','abilitySaveCSV'])$(id).disabled=!book.races.length;for(const id of ['abilityCards','abilityRanking','abilityLines','abilityWarnings'])$(id).replaceChildren();for(const id of ['abilityRaceInfo','abilityRule','abilityWarningCount'])$(id).textContent='';const r=current();if(!r){$('abilityCards').append(el('p','この競技の登録はありません。','muted'));return;}const c=r.raceCard;$('abilityRaceInfo').textContent=[c.raceId,c.date||'日付欠損',c.venue||'会場欠損',c.expectedCount+'名',conditionText(c),'出走表出所: '+(c.source||'未確認'),'確認: '+(c.checkedAt||'未確認')].join(' / ');renderLines(r);$('abilityRule').textContent=r.scoringRule.id+' v'+r.scoringRule.version+' / '+r.scoringRule.description;
  const warnings=A.warnings(r);for(const e of c.entries){const a=U.assess(r,e.number);if(a.kind==='LEGACY'||a.coverage<100)warnings.push(e.number+'番: '+(a.kind==='LEGACY'?'旧/独自ルール・根拠率未算出':'根拠率'+a.coverage+'% / 不足 '+a.missing.join('、')));}for(const w of warnings)$('abilityWarnings').append(el('li',w));$('abilityWarningCount').textContent=warnings.length?'不足・未確認 '+warnings.length+'項目（詳細を展開できます）':'入力上の不足警告なし。実データ校正は未実施です。';
  const table=el('table'),caption=el('caption','全出走者の暫定比較（根拠率の異なる順位は参考）');table.append(caption);const head=el('thead'),hr=el('tr');for(const h of ['仮順位','番号・名前／詳細','能力点','根拠率'])hr.append(el('th',h));head.append(hr);table.append(head);const body=el('tbody');const rows=U.ranked(r,$('abilitySort').value);if(!rows.some(e=>e.number===selected))selected=rows[0]?.number;
  for(const e of rows){const tr=el('tr');tr.dataset.number=e.number;tr.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(e.number===selected));const name=el('td'),b=el('button',e.number+'番 '+(e.name||'名前欠損'));b.dataset.detail=e.number;b.addEventListener('click',()=>{selected=e.number;for(const row of body.children)row.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(Number(row.dataset.number)===selected));renderDetail(r,selected);$('abilityCards').tabIndex=-1;$('abilityCards').focus();});name.append(b);tr.append(el('td',e.rank===null?'—':e.rank+'位'),name,el('td',e.evaluation.score===null?'未評価':e.evaluation.score+'点'+(e.edited?' *':'')),el('td',e.assessment.coverage===null?'未算出':e.assessment.coverage+'%'));body.append(tr);}table.append(body);$('abilityRanking').append(table);renderDetail(r,selected);
 }
 function prepare(){try{stage=U.preview(book,$('abilityInput').value);$('abilityPreviewInfo').textContent=stage.incoming.races.length+'レース / '+stage.incoming.races.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.raceCard.expectedCount,0)+'名（SYNTHETIC）';$('abilityPreviewRows').replaceChildren();for(const r of stage.incoming.races){$('abilityPreviewRows').append(el('h4',r.raceCard.raceId),el('p',conditionText(r.raceCard)+' / 出所: '+(r.raceCard.source||'未確認')));if(r.raceCard.sport==='keirin')$('abilityPreviewRows').append(el('p',r.raceCard.lines?'ライン ['+r.raceCard.lines.status+']: '+r.raceCard.lines.groups.map(g=>g.join(' → ')).join(' / '):'ライン未確認'));const ul=el('ul');for(const e of U.ranked(r))ul.append(el('li',(e.rank===null?'未評価':e.rank+'位')+' · '+e.number+'番 '+e.name+' / '+(e.evaluation.score===null?'未評価':e.evaluation.score+'点')+' / 根拠率 '+(e.assessment.coverage===null?'未算出':e.assessment.coverage+'%')));$('abilityPreviewRows').append(ul);}$('abilityPreview').hidden=false;$('abilityCommit').disabled=false;status('検証しました。まだ登録していません。プレビューを確認して確定追加してください。');}catch(e){invalidate();status(e.message,true);}}
 function openEditor(k,n){const r=book.races.find(r=>key(r)===k),v=U.effective(r,n);editTarget={key:k,number:n};$('abilityEditName').textContent=r.raceCard.raceId+' / '+n+'番 '+v.name;$('abilityEditScore').value=v.score===null?'':v.score;$('abilityEditReason').value=v.reason;$('abilityEditContext').value=v.contextNote;$('abilityEditor').hidden=false;$('abilityEditScore').focus();}
 function save(name,text,mime){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type:mime})),a=el('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);status(name+' を保存します。同期対象外の保存先を選んでください。');}
 $('abilityInput').addEventListener('input',()=>{invalidate();status('入力が変わりました。プレビューで検証してください。');});$('abilityImport').addEventListener('click',prepare);
 $('abilityCommit').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{const chosen=key(stage.incoming.races[0]),next=U.commit(book,$('abilityInput').value,stage);book=next;invalidate();selected=null;editTarget=null;$('abilityEditor').hidden=true;$('abilitySport').value='all';render(chosen);status('確定追加しました。JSON保存で手元に保管してください。');}catch(e){invalidate();status(e.message,true);}});
 for(const[id,sport]of [['abilityHorseSample','horse'],['abilityKeirinSample','keirin']])$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{invalidate();$('abilityInput').value=JSON.stringify(U.sample(sport),null,2);prepare();});
 $('abilityFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;invalidate();const token=revision;try{if(f.size>U.MAX_BYTES)throw Error('UTF-8 1MB以内です');const s=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer());if(token!==revision)return;$('abilityInput').value=s;prepare();}catch(err){if(token===revision)status('ファイルを確認してください: '+err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}});
 for(const id of ['abilitySport','abilityRace'])$(id).addEventListener('change',()=>{selected=null;editTarget=null;$('abilityEditor').hidden=true;render();});$('abilitySort').addEventListener('change',()=>render());
 $('abilityEditSave').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!editTarget)return;try{const raw=$('abilityEditScore').value.trim();if($('abilityEditScore').validity.badInput)throw Error('点数が不正です');const after={score:raw===''?null:Number(raw),reason:$('abilityEditReason').value,contextNote:$('abilityEditContext').value};const next=U.edit(book,editTarget.key,editTarget.number,after,new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/,'Z'));U.exportJSON(next);U.toCSV(next);book=next;invalidate();render(editTarget.key);$('abilityEditor').hidden=true;editTarget=null;status('USER修正と履歴を保存しました。原評価は保持しています。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}});$('abilityEditCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('abilityEditor').hidden=true;editTarget=null;});
 for(const[id,format]of [['abilitySaveJSON','json'],['abilitySaveCSV','csv']])$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{try{save('racelab_ability_synthetic.'+format,format==='json'?U.exportJSON(book):U.toCSV(book),format==='json'?'application/json;charset=utf-8':'text/csv;charset=utf-8');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}});
 function renderPrompt(){$('abilityPrompt').value=U.prompt($('abilityPromptSport').value);}renderPrompt();$('abilityPromptSport').addEventListener('change',renderPrompt);
 $('abilityCopyPrompt').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{if(!navigator.clipboard?.writeText)throw Error('selection fallback');await navigator.clipboard.writeText($('abilityPrompt').value);status('分析プロンプトをコピーしました。');}catch{$('abilityPrompt').focus();$('abilityPrompt').select();status('依頼文を選択しました。コピー操作（Ctrl+C／長押し）で取得してください。');}});
 $('abilitySavePrompt').addEventListener('click',()=>save('uni_'+$('abilityPromptSport').value+'_prompt.md',$('abilityPrompt').value,'text/markdown;charset=utf-8'));$('abilityTemplate').addEventListener('click',()=>save('uni_'+$('abilityPromptSport').value+'_sample.json',JSON.stringify(U.sample($('abilityPromptSport').value),null,2),'application/json;charset=utf-8'));
 const old=['showEV','showHistory','showQuality','showModel'];$('showAbility').addEventListener('click',()=>{for(const id of ['phase1View','historyView','qualityView','modelView'])$(id).hidden=true;for(const id of old)$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('abilityView').hidden=false;$('showAbility').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('abilityHeading').focus();});for(const id of old)$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{$('abilityView').hidden=true;$('showAbility').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});render();$('showAbility').click();
})();

</script>
<script id="manual-ui">
'use strict';
(()=>{
 const M=globalThis.RaceManualCore,S=globalThis.RaceManualAssets,Q=globalThis.RaceQualityCore,$=id=>document.getElementById(id);const states={REAL:null,TEST_FIXTURE:null};let policy=null,stage=null,selected=null,editing=null,revision=0,gateHash=null;
 const kind=()=>$('manualKind').value,book=()=>states[kind()],ctx=()=>({now:new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/,'Z'),storagePath:$('manualStorage').value,confirmedUnsynced:$('manualUnsynced').checked});
 const el=(tag,text,cls)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;if(cls)e.className=cls;return e;};
 const current=()=>book()?.analysis.races.find(r=>r.sport+'|'+r.raceId===$('manualRace').value);
 function status(s,error=false){$('manualError').textContent=error?s:'';$('manualStatus').textContent=error?'処理を保留しました。登録済みデータは保持されています。':s;}
 function invalidate(lock=false){stage=null;revision++;$('manualCommit').disabled=true;$('manualPreview').hidden=true;if(lock){policy=null;gateHash=null;$('manualPayload').disabled=true;$('manualGateStatus').textContent='条件を再確認してください。';}updateExports();}
 function check(){if(!policy||gateHash!==Q.hash({policy,storage:ctx().storagePath,unsynced:ctx().confirmedUnsynced,kind:kind()}))throw Error('許諾台帳・保存条件を再確認してください');const g=M.gate(policy,ctx());if(!g.allowed)throw Error('HOLD: '+g.reasons.join(' / '));return g;}
 function updateExports(){let okay=false,ai=false;try{const g=check();if(book()){M.bookValidate(book(),ctx(),kind());okay=true;ai=g.externalAIEligible&&policy.analysisMethod==='EXTERNAL_AI';}}catch{}for(const id of ['manualJSON','manualCSV','manualTXT'])$(id).disabled=!okay;$('manualCopy').disabled=!okay||!ai;$('manualCopyNotice').textContent=ai?'外部AI利用条件を利用者が確認済みと申告。コピーは手動操作のみで、自動送信はしません。':'外部AI用途が未確認/利用対象外のため、ユニ向け一括コピーは無効です。ローカル保存は許可範囲内だけで利用できます。';}
 function render(){const b=book();$('manualKindNotice').textContent=kind()==='REAL'?'REAL：実レースの手動分析専用。架空データと混ぜません。':'TEST_FIXTURE：架空資料の機能検証。実レースとして扱いません。';$('manualCount').textContent=(b?.analysis.races.length||0)+'レース / '+(b?.analysis.races.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.count,0)||0)+'名';const old=$('manualRace').value;$('manualRace').replaceChildren();for(const r of b?.analysis.races||[]){const o=el('option',(r.sport==='horse'?'競馬':'競輪')+' / '+r.raceName);o.value=r.sport+'|'+r.raceId;$('manualRace').append(o);}if(b?.analysis.races.some(r=>r.sport+'|'+r.raceId===old))$('manualRace').value=old;for(const id of ['manualRanking','manualDetails','manualLines'])$(id).replaceChildren();$('manualRaceInfo').textContent='';$('manualText').value='';const r=current();if(!r){updateExports();return;}$('manualRaceInfo').textContent=[r.raceName,r.date||'日付未確認',r.venue||'会場未確認',(r.raceNo??'未確認')+'R',r.raceId,M.conditionsText(r)].join(' / ');if(r.lines){$('manualLines').append(el('p',r.lines.status==='PREDICTED'?'ライン予想（未確定）':r.lines.status==='CONFIRMED_BY_USER'?'確認済みとの利用者申告（独立未確認）':'ライン未確認','ability-line-meta'));for(const g of r.lines.groups)$('manualLines').append(el('div',g.join(' → '),'ability-line'));for(const ref of r.lines.refs)$('manualLines').append(el('p','出典: '+ref.sourceId+' / '+ref.reference+' / '+ref.checkedAt,'ability-line-meta'));}
  const rows=M.ranked(b,r);if(!rows.some(e=>e.runner.number===selected))selected=rows[0]?.runner.number;const table=el('table'),head=el('thead'),tr=el('tr');for(const h of ['仮順位','番号・名前／詳細','総合点','根拠率'])tr.append(el('th',h));head.append(tr);table.append(head);const body=el('tbody');for(const row of rows){const tr=el('tr');tr.dataset.number=row.runner.number;const td=el('td'),button=el('button',row.runner.number+'番 '+row.runner.name);button.dataset.manualDetail=row.runner.number;button.addEventListener('click',()=>{selected=row.runner.number;detail(r,selected);$('manualDetails').tabIndex=-1;$('manualDetails').focus();});td.append(button);tr.append(el('td',row.rank===null?'—':row.rank+'位'),td,el('td',row.effective.score===null?'未評価':row.effective.score+'点'),el('td',row.assessment.coverage+'%'));body.append(tr);}table.append(body);$('manualRanking').append(table);detail(r,selected);try{$('manualText').value=M.toText(b,ctx(),kind());}catch(e){$('manualText').value='現在の許諾ゲートでは出力保留: '+e.message;}updateExports();
 }
 function dl(pairs){const d=el('dl');for(const[k,v]of pairs)d.append(el('dt',k),el('dd',v===null||v===''?'未確認':String(v)));return d;}
 function detail(r,n){const b=book(),row=M.ranked(b,r).find(x=>x.runner.number===n);$('manualDetails').replaceChildren();if(!row)return;const a=row.assessment,runner=row.runner,v=row.effective,card=el('article',undefined,'ability-card');card.append(el('h3',n+'番 '+runner.name),el('p',(v.score===null?'未評価':v.score+'点')+' / 根拠率'+a.coverage+'% / 評価済'+a.assessed+'/6'),el('p','不足: '+(a.missing.join('、')||'なし'),'warn'));if(r.lines){const g=r.lines.groups.find(g=>g.includes(n));card.append(el('p','ライン位置: '+(g?(r.lines.status!=='CONFIRMED_BY_USER'?'想定':'')+(g.length===1?'単騎':g.indexOf(n)===0?'先頭':(g.indexOf(n)+1)+'番手'):'未記載')));}if(b.edits.some(e=>e.raceKey===r.sport+'|'+r.raceId&&e.number===n))card.append(el('p','USER手修正あり。計算参考点 '+a.score+'点 / 修正理由: '+v.reason,'warn'));card.append(el('p','推定区分: '+(runner.evaluation.kind==='AI_ESTIMATE'?'AI推定':'利用者推定')+' / 条件・展開所見: '+(v.contextNote||'未確認')));
  for(const item of a.items){const d=el('details',undefined,'ability-evidence-item');d.open=item.id==='recentPerformance';d.dataset.manualItem=item.id;d.append(el('summary',item.label+' / 重み'+item.weight+'% / '+(item.score===null?'未評価':item.score+'点')),dl([['推定評価',item.reason],['未確認',item.unknowns]]));for(const fid of item.factIds){const f=runner.facts.find(f=>f.id===fid);d.append(dl([[f.status==='CONFIRMED_BY_USER'?'確認事実（利用者申告）':'未確認の記載',f.text],['情報区分',f.ref?.origin==='OFFICIAL_DECLARED'?'公式情報として利用者申告（独立未確認）':f.ref?'利用者申告':'情報区分未確認'],['確認状態',f.status],['出典',f.ref?f.ref.sourceId+' / '+f.ref.reference:'未確認'],['確認時刻',f.ref?.checkedAt||'未確認']]));}card.append(d);}
  const refs=el('details');refs.append(el('summary','レース出典・許諾確認申告・安定ID'),el('p','安定ID: '+(runner.stableId??'欠損（番号で代用しません）')));for(const ref of r.cardRefs)refs.append(el('p',ref.sourceId+' / '+ref.reference+' / '+ref.checkedAt));for(const s of b.policy.sources)refs.append(el('p',s.label+' ['+s.kind+'] / 規約: '+s.termsReference+' / 確認者: '+s.reviewer+' / '+s.reviewedAt+' / 提供元の正式許諾を認証していません。'));card.append(refs);const history=el('details');history.append(el('summary','修正履歴'));for(const e of b.edits.filter(e=>e.raceKey===r.sport+'|'+r.raceId&&e.number===n))history.append(el('p','USER '+e.editedAt+' '+e.ruleId+' v'+e.ruleVersion+'\n修正前: '+JSON.stringify(e.before)+'\n修正後: '+JSON.stringify(e.after)));card.append(history);const edit=el('button','総合点・理由を手修正');edit.dataset.manualEdit=n;edit.addEventListener('click',()=>{try{check();editing={key:r.sport+'|'+r.raceId,number:n};$('manualEditName').textContent=r.raceName+' / '+runner.name;$('manualEditScore').value=v.score??'';$('manualEditReason').value=v.reason;$('manualEditContext').value=v.contextNote;$('manualEditor').hidden=false;$('manualEditScore').focus();}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}});card.append(edit);$('manualDetails').append(card);
 }
 function confirmGate(){try{invalidate(true);const p=M.policyValidate(M.parseJSON($('manualPolicy').value));if(p.dataKind!==kind())throw Error('選択区分と台帳区分が一致しません');const g=M.gate(p,ctx());if(!g.allowed)throw Error('HOLD: '+g.reasons.join(' / '));policy=p;gateHash=Q.hash({policy,storage:ctx().storagePath,unsynced:ctx().confirmedUnsynced,kind:kind()});$('manualPayload').disabled=false;$('manualGateStatus').textContent='利用者の条件確認申告を確認。正式許諾の認証ではありません。クラウド送信は無効。';status('分析データを貼り付け、プレビューしてください。');render();}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}}
 function preview(){try{check();stage=M.preview(book(),policy,$('manualInput').value,ctx(),kind());$('manualPreview').replaceChildren(el('h3','登録前プレビュー：'+kind()),el('p','まだ登録していません。'));for(const r of stage.next.analysis.races){$('manualPreview').append(el('h4',r.raceName));for(const row of M.ranked(stage.next,r))$('manualPreview').append(el('p',row.runner.number+'番 '+row.runner.name+' / '+(row.effective.score===null?'未評価':row.effective.score+'点')+' / 根拠率'+row.assessment.coverage+'%'));}$('manualPreview').hidden=false;$('manualCommit').disabled=false;status('条件と内容を検証しました。確認して確定追加してください。');}catch(e){invalidate();status(e.message,true);}}
 function save(name,text,type){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type})),a=el('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);status('保存先を同期対象外に指定してください: '+name);}
 $('manualKind').addEventListener('change',()=>{invalidate(true);$('manualPolicy').value='';$('manualInput').value='';editing=null;selected=null;$('manualEditor').hidden=true;render();status('区分を切り替えました。保存データは合算しません。');});for(const id of ['manualPolicy','manualStorage','manualUnsynced'])$(id).addEventListener(id==='manualUnsynced'?'change':'input',()=>invalidate(true));$('manualGate').addEventListener('click',confirmGate);$('manualInput').addEventListener('input',()=>invalidate());$('manualPreviewButton').addEventListener('click',preview);
 $('manualCommit').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{check();states[kind()]=M.commit(book(),policy,$('manualInput').value,stage,ctx(),kind());invalidate();selected=null;render();status('現在の区分へ確定追加しました。ローカル保存してください。');}catch(e){invalidate();status(e.message,true);}});
 for(const[id,sport]of [['manualHorseFixture','horse'],['manualKeirinFixture','keirin']])$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{const f=S.fixture(sport);$('manualKind').value='TEST_FIXTURE';invalidate(true);$('manualPolicy').value=JSON.stringify(f.policy,null,2);$('manualInput').value=JSON.stringify(f.analysis,null,2);render();confirmGate();if(policy)preview();});
 $('manualPolicyTemplate').addEventListener('click',()=>{invalidate(true);const p=S.policy(kind());for(const source of p.sources){for(const k of ['reviewer','reviewedAt','validUntil','retentionUntil','allowedLocalRoot'])source[k]='';for(const v of Object.values(source.permissions)){v.status='UNCONFIRMED';v.evidenceReference='';}}$('manualPolicy').value=JSON.stringify(p,null,2);status('全て未確認の台帳を表示しました。根拠を確認して記入してください。');});
 $('manualPolicyFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;invalidate(true);const token=revision;try{if(f.size>M.MAX)throw Error('2MB以内です');const d=M.parseJSON(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer()));if(token!==revision)return;const p=M.policyValidate(d.format===M.BF?d.policy:d);if(p.dataKind!==kind())throw Error('選択区分と台帳が不一致');$('manualPolicy').value=JSON.stringify(p,null,2);status('台帳だけを表示しました。内容を確認して「条件と保存範囲を確認」を押してください。');}catch(err){status(err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}});
 $('manualFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;invalidate();const token=revision;try{check();if(f.size>M.MAX)throw Error('2MB以内です');const text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer());if(token!==revision)return;$('manualInput').value=text;preview();}catch(err){status(err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}});
 $('manualRace').addEventListener('change',()=>{selected=null;render();});$('manualEditCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{editing=null;$('manualEditor').hidden=true;});$('manualEditSave').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!editing)return;try{check();if($('manualEditScore').validity.badInput)throw Error('点数不正');const s=$('manualEditScore').value.trim(),c=ctx(),next=M.edit(book(),editing.key,editing.number,{score:s===''?null:Number(s),reason:$('manualEditReason').value,contextNote:$('manualEditContext').value},c.now,c,kind());M.toJSON(next,c,kind());M.toCSV(next,c,kind());states[kind()]=next;invalidate();render();editing=null;$('manualEditor').hidden=true;status('USER修正と履歴を保存しました。原評価は保持しています。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}});
 for(const[id,extension]of [['manualJSON','json'],['manualCSV','csv'],['manualTXT','txt']])$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{try{check();const text=extension==='json'?M.toJSON(book(),ctx(),kind()):extension==='csv'?M.toCSV(book(),ctx(),kind()):M.toText(book(),ctx(),kind());save('racelab_manual_'+kind()+'.'+extension,text,extension==='json'?'application/json;charset=utf-8':extension==='csv'?'text/csv;charset=utf-8':'text/plain;charset=utf-8');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}});
 $('manualCopy').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{const g=check();if(!g.externalAIEligible||policy.analysisMethod!=='EXTERNAL_AI')throw Error('外部AI用途は未確認/対象外');const text=M.toText(book(),ctx(),kind())+'\n\n```json\n'+JSON.stringify(book().analysis,null,2)+'\n```';$('manualText').value=text;if(navigator.clipboard?.writeText){try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);status('分析文とJSONをコピーしました。自動送信はしていません。');return;}catch{}}$('manualText').parentElement.open=true;$('manualText').focus();$('manualText').select();status('一括出力を選択しました。Ctrl+C/長押しでコピーしてください。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}});
 const prompt=()=>$('manualPrompt').value=S.shortPrompt($('manualPromptSport').value);$('manualPromptSport').addEventListener('change',prompt);prompt();$('manualCopyPrompt').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const text=$('manualPrompt').value;try{if(!navigator.clipboard?.writeText)throw Error('select');await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);status('実データを含まない短い依頼文をコピーしました。');}catch{$('manualPrompt').focus();$('manualPrompt').select();status('短い依頼文を選択しました。Ctrl+C/長押しでコピーしてください。');}});$('manualSavePrompt').addEventListener('click',()=>save('uni_'+$('manualPromptSport').value+'_short_prompt.md',$('manualPrompt').value,'text/markdown;charset=utf-8'));$('manualTemplate').addEventListener('click',()=>save('manual_'+$('manualPromptSport').value+'_empty_V2.json',JSON.stringify(S.template($('manualPromptSport').value),null,2),'application/json;charset=utf-8'));$('manualRepair').addEventListener('click',()=>save('uni_V2_repair_prompt.md',S.repairPrompt(),'text/markdown;charset=utf-8'));
 const tabs=['showAbility','showEV','showHistory','showQuality','showModel'];$('showManual').addEventListener('click',()=>{for(const id of ['abilityView','phase1View','historyView','qualityView','modelView'])$(id).hidden=true;for(const id of tabs)$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('manualView').hidden=false;$('showManual').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('manualHeading').focus();updateExports();});for(const id of tabs)$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{$('manualView').hidden=true;$('showManual').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});render();$('showManual').click();
})();

<script id="prep-ui">
'use strict';
(()=>{
 const P=RacePreparationCore,M=RaceManualCore,S=RaceManualAssets,U=RaceUniCore,H=RaceHistoryCore,Q=RaceQualityCore,$=id=>document.getElementById(id),clone=x=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
 let draft=null,stage=null,requestStage=null,local=false,revision=0,lastError='';const buffers={REAL:null,TEST_FIXTURE:null};
 const ctx=()=>({now:new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/,'Z'),storagePath:$('prepStorage').value,confirmedUnsynced:$('prepUnsynced').checked});
 const node=(tag,value)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(value!==undefined)e.textContent=value;return e;};
 function notice(s,error=false){$('prepMessage').textContent=error?'処理を保留しました。登録済みV2は保持されています。':s;$('prepError').textContent=error?s:'';if(error)lastError=s;}
 function invalidate(){revision++;stage=null;requestStage=null;for(const id of ['prepTransfer','prepCopyRequest','prepSaveRequest','prepSaveArchive'])$(id).disabled=true;$('prepRequestText').value='';$('prepResponsePreview').replaceChildren();$('prepReport').value='';}
 function control(parent,label,value,type='text',options=null){const l=node('label',label),e=node(options?'select':type==='textarea'?'textarea':'input');if(options)for(const v of options){const o=node('option',v);o.value=v;e.append(o);}else if(type!=='textarea')e.type=type;else e.rows=2;e.value=value??'';l.append(e);parent.append(l);return e;}
 const nullable=s=>s.trim()===''?null:s.trim();
 const metadata=[];
 function makeMeta(parent,key,ref,t){const d=node('details'),title=node('summary','出典・事実区分・時刻を確認/修正');d.append(title);const grid=node('div');grid.className='prep-grid prep-fact-meta';d.append(grid);const fields={};for(const[k,label]of [['sourceId','sourceId'],['reference','参照箇所'],['checkedAt','確認時刻']])fields[k]=control(grid,label,ref?.[k]);fields.origin=control(grid,'原出所の申告',ref?.origin||'USER_DECLARED','text',['USER_DECLARED','OFFICIAL_DECLARED']);fields.nature=control(grid,'記載の区分',t?.nature||'UNKNOWN','text',['UNKNOWN','FACT','ESTIMATE']);fields.phase=control(grid,'対象情報の時点',t?.phase||'UNKNOWN','text',['UNKNOWN','PRE_RACE','POST_RACE']);for(const[k,label]of [['publishedAt','公表時刻'],['acquiredAt','取得時刻'],['availableAt','利用可能時刻']])fields[k]=control(grid,label,t?.[k]);parent.append(d);metadata.push({key,fields});}
 function readMeta(entry){const f=entry.fields,v=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(f).map(([k,e])=>[k,e.value]));return {ref:v.sourceId.trim()&&v.reference.trim()&&v.checkedAt.trim()?{sourceId:v.sourceId.trim(),reference:v.reference,checkedAt:v.checkedAt,origin:v.origin}:null,timing:{key:entry.key,nature:v.nature,phase:v.phase,publishedAt:nullable(v.publishedAt),acquiredAt:nullable(v.acquiredAt),availableAt:nullable(v.availableAt)}};}
 const factEditors=[];
 function render(p){for(const id of ['prepResponse','prepRepairText','prepCommonSource','prepCommonReference','prepCommonChecked','prepCommonPublished','prepCommonAcquired','prepCommonAvailable','prepSourceLabel','prepSourceReference','prepTerms','prepReviewer','prepReviewed','prepValid','prepRetention','prepRoot','prepPermissionEvidence'])$(id).value='';$('prepCommonAttest').checked=false;for(const id of ['prepLocalAnalysis','prepLocalStorage','prepExternalAI'])$(id).value='UNCONFIRMED';draft=clone(p);$('prepKind').value=p.dataKind;$('prepSport').value=p.race.sport;$('prepCount').value=p.race.count;for(const[id,k]of [['prepRaceId','raceId'],['prepRaceName','raceName'],['prepDate','date'],['prepVenue','venue'],['prepRaceNo','raceNo']])$(id).value=p.race[k]??'';$('prepCutoff').value=p.cutoffAt??'';$('prepStarts').value=p.startsAt??'';$('prepPolicy').value=JSON.stringify(p.policy,null,2);$('prepRosterComplete').checked=p.rosterComplete;$('prepUnknowns').value=p.unknowns.join('\n');$('prepRoster').value=H.serializeCSV(p.race.runners.map(r=>[String(r.number),r.name,r.stableId??''])).replace(/^\uFEFF/,'');$('prepConditions').replaceChildren();for(const k of Object.keys(p.race.conditions)){const labels={distanceMetres:'距離(m)',surface:'馬場区分',going:'馬場状態',bankLengthMetres:'バンク長(m)',raceClass:'クラス'};const e=control($('prepConditions'),labels[k]||k,p.race.conditions[k]);e.dataset.prepCondition=k;}
 metadata.length=0;factEditors.length=0;$('prepCardTiming').replaceChildren();makeMeta($('prepCardTiming'),'card:0',p.race.cardRefs[0],p.timing.find(t=>t.key==='card:0'));$('prepRunners').replaceChildren();for(const r of p.race.runners){const card=node('details');card.dataset.prepRunner=r.number;card.append(node('summary',r.number+'番 '+(r.name||'名前未入力')+' / 全6能力項目'));const info=node('p','安定ID: '+(r.stableId??'不明（番号で代用しません）'));card.append(info);for(const item of U.RULES[p.race.sport].items){const facts=r.facts.filter(f=>f.topic===item.id);if(!facts.length)facts.push({id:'prep-'+r.number+'-'+item.id,topic:item.id,text:'',status:'UNCONFIRMED',ref:null});for(const fact of facts){const box=node('div'),field=control(box,item.label+'（記載事実。AI推定は下で区分）',fact.text,'textarea');field.dataset.prepFact=fact.id;makeMeta(box,'fact:'+fact.id,fact.ref,p.timing.find(t=>t.key==='fact:'+fact.id));const meta=metadata[metadata.length-1];factEditors.push({number:r.number,fact:clone(fact),field,meta});card.append(box);}}$('prepRunners').append(card);}
 const line=p.race.lines;$('prepLineSection').hidden=p.race.sport!=='keirin';$('prepLineGroups').value=line?.groups.map(g=>g.join('-')).join(' / ')||'';$('prepLineStatus').value=line?.status||'UNCONFIRMED';$('prepLineNote').value=line?.note||'';$('prepLineTiming').replaceChildren();if(p.race.sport==='keirin')makeMeta($('prepLineTiming'),'line:0',line?.refs[0],p.timing.find(t=>t.key==='line:0'));$('prepQuality').replaceChildren();invalidate();}
 function collect(){if(!draft)throw Error('空レースまたは架空資料を作成してください');const p=clone(draft);if(Number($('prepCount').value)!==p.race.count)throw Error('出走数を変更したら空レースを作成してください');p.policy=M.policyValidate(M.parseJSON($('prepPolicy').value));if(p.dataKind!==$('prepKind').value||p.race.sport!==$('prepSport').value)throw Error('区分/競技の切替後はレースを新規作成してください');for(const[id,k]of [['prepRaceId','raceId'],['prepRaceName','raceName'],['prepDate','date'],['prepVenue','venue']])p.race[k]=$(id).value;p.race.raceNo=nullable($('prepRaceNo').value)===null?null:Number($('prepRaceNo').value);p.cutoffAt=nullable($('prepCutoff').value);p.startsAt=nullable($('prepStarts').value);p.rosterComplete=$('prepRosterComplete').checked;p.unknowns=$('prepUnknowns').value.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());for(const e of document.querySelectorAll('[data-prep-condition]'))p.race.conditions[e.dataset.prepCondition]=nullable(e.value)===null?null:e.dataset.prepCondition.endsWith('Metres')?Number(e.value):e.value;
 p.timing=[];const card=readMeta(metadata.find(x=>x.key==='card:0'));p.race.cardRefs=card.ref?[card.ref,...draft.race.cardRefs.slice(1)]:[];if(card.ref){p.timing.push(card.timing);p.timing.push(...draft.timing.filter(t=>/^card:[1-9]/.test(t.key)));}for(const r of p.race.runners)r.facts=[];for(const e of factEditors){if(!e.field.value.trim())continue;const m=readMeta(e.meta),f={...e.fact,text:e.field.value,status:m.timing.nature==='FACT'&&m.ref?'CONFIRMED_BY_USER':'UNCONFIRMED',ref:m.ref};p.race.runners.find(r=>r.number===e.number).facts.push(f);p.timing.push(m.timing);}
 if(p.race.sport==='keirin'){const value=$('prepLineGroups').value.trim(),m=readMeta(metadata.find(x=>x.key==='line:0'));p.race.lines=value||$('prepLineNote').value||m.ref?{groups:value?value.split('/').map(g=>g.trim().split(/[-→]/).map(n=>Number(n.trim()))):[],status:$('prepLineStatus').value,refs:m.ref?[m.ref,...(draft.race.lines?.refs.slice(1)||[])]:[],note:$('prepLineNote').value}:null;if(m.ref){p.timing.push(m.timing);p.timing.push(...draft.timing.filter(t=>/^line:[1-9]/.test(t.key)));}}return P.validate(p);}
 function inspect(p){const q=P.inspect(p,ctx());$('prepQuality').replaceChildren(node('h4','品質点検: '+p.dataKind),node('p',q.totals.entrants+'名 / 事前根拠 '+q.totals.evidenced+'/'+q.totals.items+'項目 / 充足度 '+q.evidenceCoverage+'%（予測精度ではありません）'));for(const[label,list]of [['HOLD（解決が必要）',q.critical],['不足・未確認',q.missing],['依頼から除外される情報',q.excluded.map(e=>e.key+': '+e.reasons.join('、'))]]){const details=node('details');details.open=!!list.length;details.append(node('summary',label+' '+list.length+'件'));const ul=node('ul');ul.className='prep-missing';for(const s of list)ul.append(node('li',s));details.append(ul);$('prepQuality').append(details);}return q;}
 function save(name,text,type='text/plain;charset=utf-8'){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type})),a=node('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);notice('同期対象外の指定フォルダへ保存してください: '+name);}
 async function copy(id){const t=$(id);try{if(!navigator.clipboard?.writeText)throw Error('select');await navigator.clipboard.writeText(t.value);notice('コピーしました。送信は行っていません。');}catch{t.focus();t.select();notice('出力を選択しました。Ctrl+C/長押しでコピーしてください。');}}
 function preview(p,text,external){const s=P.response(p,text,ctx(),external);stage={...s,prepHash:Q.hash(p),rawHash:Q.sha256(text),external};$('prepResponsePreview').replaceChildren(node('h4','照合済みプレビュー（まだ未登録）'));for(const r of s.analysis.races){const table=node('table'),tr=node('tr');for(const h of ['仮順位','番号・名前','能力点','根拠率'])tr.append(node('th',h));table.append(tr);for(const row of M.ranked(s.book,r)){const tr=node('tr');for(const v of [row.rank??'未評価',row.runner.number+' '+row.runner.name,row.effective.score??'未評価',row.assessment.coverage+'%'])tr.append(node('td',v));table.append(tr);}$('prepResponsePreview').append(table);if(r.lines)$('prepResponsePreview').append(node('p','ライン '+r.lines.status+': '+r.lines.groups.map(g=>g.join(' → ')).join(' / ')));for(const row of M.ranked(s.book,r)){if(row.assessment.coverage<100)$('prepResponsePreview').append(node('p',row.runner.number+'番 根拠不足: '+row.assessment.missing.join('、')));}}$('prepReport').value=P.report(s.book,ctx(),p.dataKind);$('prepTransfer').disabled=false;$('prepSaveArchive').disabled=false;notice('準備した全出走者・根拠・採点を照合しました。比較画面で確定できます。');}
 $('prepNew').onclick=()=>{try{const n=Number($('prepCount').value),sport=$('prepSport').value;if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<1||n>(sport==='horse'?18:9)||sport==='keirin'&&![7,9].includes(n))throw Error('競馬1〜18頭・競輪7/9車を指定してください');render(P.scaffold(sport,n,$('prepKind').value));notice('空欄は未確認のまま入力してください。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 for(const[id,sport,count]of [['prepHorse18','horse',18],['prepKeirin7','keirin',7],['prepKeirin9','keirin',9]])$(id).onclick=()=>{render(P.fixture(sport,count));$('prepStorage').value='C:\\RaceLabLocal\\manual';$('prepUnsynced').checked=true;notice('独自架空資料を表示しました。実在情報や実AIの採点ではありません。');};
 $('prepKind').onchange=()=>{const previous=draft?.dataKind;if(previous){try{buffers[previous]=collectForSwitch();}catch{buffers[previous]=clone(draft);}}const k=$('prepKind').value;render(buffers[k]||P.scaffold($('prepSport').value,Number($('prepCount').value),k));$('prepResponse').value='';$('prepUnsynced').checked=false;notice('区分を分離して切り替えました。許諾・同期先を再確認してください。');};
 function collectForSwitch(){const k=$('prepKind').value;$('prepKind').value=draft.dataKind;try{return collect();}finally{$('prepKind').value=k;}}
 $('prepSourceApply').onclick=()=>{try{const p=S.policy($('prepKind').value,'USER_ONLY'),s=p.sources[0];for(const[k,id]of [['label','prepSourceLabel'],['reference','prepSourceReference'],['termsReference','prepTerms'],['reviewer','prepReviewer'],['reviewedAt','prepReviewed'],['validUntil','prepValid'],['retentionUntil','prepRetention'],['allowedLocalRoot','prepRoot']])s[k]=$(id).value;for(const[k,id]of [['localAnalysis','prepLocalAnalysis'],['localStorage','prepLocalStorage'],['externalAI','prepExternalAI']])s.permissions[k]={status:$(id).value,evidenceReference:$('prepPermissionEvidence').value};s.permissions.cloudStorage={status:'UNCONFIRMED',evidenceReference:''};$('prepPolicy').value=JSON.stringify(M.policyValidate(p),null,2);invalidate();notice('1資料の台帳を表示しました。条件確認の申告であり正式認証ではありません。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepApplyRoster').onclick=()=>{try{const p=collect(),rows=H.parseCSV($('prepRoster').value);if(rows.length!==p.race.count)throw Error('出走数と一覧の人数が不一致');const used=new Set();const runners=rows.map(c=>{if(c.length<2||c.length>3)throw Error('番号,名前,安定IDの2〜3列を指定');const number=Number(c[0]);if(!Number.isInteger(number)||number<1||number>(p.race.sport==='horse'?18:p.race.count)||used.has(number)||!c[1].trim())throw Error('番号重複/範囲/名前が不正');used.add(number);const old=p.race.runners.find(r=>r.number===number),r=clone(old||P.scaffold(p.race.sport,1,p.dataKind).race.runners[0]);r.number=number;r.name=c[1];r.stableId=nullable(c[2]||'');if(r.stableId===String(number))throw Error('安定IDに出走番号を代用できません');return r;});p.race.runners=runners;p.rosterComplete=false;render(p);notice('全員の一覧を反映しました。名簿を確認し、全員確認チェックを付けてください。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepApplyCommon').onclick=()=>{try{if(!$('prepCommonAttest').checked)throw Error('共通出典を適用できるか確認してください');const fields={sourceId:$('prepCommonSource').value,reference:$('prepCommonReference').value,checkedAt:$('prepCommonChecked').value,publishedAt:$('prepCommonPublished').value,acquiredAt:$('prepCommonAcquired').value,availableAt:$('prepCommonAvailable').value};for(const entry of metadata){if(entry.key.startsWith('fact:')&&!factEditors.find(e=>e.meta===entry)?.field.value.trim())continue;for(const[k,v]of Object.entries(fields))entry.fields[k].value=v;entry.fields.phase.value='PRE_RACE';entry.fields.nature.value=entry.key==='line:0'&&$('prepLineStatus').value==='PREDICTED'?'ESTIMATE':'FACT';}invalidate();notice('申告に基づき共通出所と時刻を適用しました。各項目を確認できます。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepCheck').onclick=()=>{try{inspect(collect());notice('不足と除外理由を表示しました。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepRequest').onclick=()=>{try{const p=collect();inspect(p);const s=P.request(p,ctx());requestStage={...s,prepHash:Q.hash(p)};local=false;$('prepRequestText').value=s.text;$('prepCopyRequest').disabled=false;$('prepSaveRequest').disabled=false;notice('依頼一式を作成。除外 '+s.removed.length+'件。台帳原本・保存先・未許諾資料は含めません。');}catch(e){invalidate();notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepLocal').onclick=()=>{try{const p=collect();inspect(p);const s=P.target(p,ctx(),false);local=true;$('prepResponse').value=JSON.stringify(s.analysis,null,2);preview(p,$('prepResponse').value,false);notice('USER_ONLYの未採点V2です。外部AIへ提示せず、比較画面で手動評価できます。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 const freshRequest=()=>{const p=collect();if(!requestStage||requestStage.prepHash!==Q.hash(p))throw Error('準備内容が変わりました。依頼を再作成してください');return P.request(p,ctx());};
 $('prepCopyRequest').onclick=async()=>{try{freshRequest();await copy('prepRequestText');}catch(e){invalidate();notice(e.message,true);}};$('prepSaveRequest').onclick=()=>{try{const s=freshRequest();save('racelab_request_'+s.analysis.dataKind+'.txt',s.text);}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepSave').onclick=()=>{try{const p=collect();save('racelab_preparation_'+p.dataKind+'.json',P.save(p,ctx()),'application/json;charset=utf-8');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepRestore').onchange=async e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;const token=revision;try{if(f.size>M.MAX)throw Error('2MiB以内です');const text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer());if(token!==revision)return;const p=P.restore(text,ctx(),$('prepKind').value);render(p);notice('ハッシュとローカル条件を検査して準備を復元しました。');}catch(err){notice(err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}};
 $('prepResponseFile').onchange=async e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;stage=null;$('prepTransfer').disabled=true;$('prepSaveArchive').disabled=true;const token=revision;try{if(f.size>M.MAX)throw Error('2MiB以内です');const text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer());if(token!==revision)return;$('prepResponse').value=text;stage=null;$('prepTransfer').disabled=true;preview(collect(),text,!local);}catch(err){notice(err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}};
 $('prepValidateResponse').onclick=()=>{try{preview(collect(),$('prepResponse').value,!local);}catch(e){stage=null;$('prepTransfer').disabled=true;$('prepSaveArchive').disabled=true;notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepSimulate').onclick=()=>{try{const p=collect();if(p.dataKind!=='TEST_FIXTURE')throw Error('模擬応答はTEST_FIXTURE専用です');local=false;$('prepResponse').value='架空の模擬分析（実AIではありません）\n```json\n'+JSON.stringify(P.simulatedResponse(p,ctx()),null,2)+'\n```';stage=null;$('prepTransfer').disabled=true;notice('架空資料の模擬応答を表示しました。照合してください。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepRepair').onclick=()=>{$('prepRepairText').value=P.repair(lastError);notice('修正依頼を表示しました。原文・資料・台帳は含めていません。必要な情報だけ許諾確認後に追記してください。');};
 function freshStage(){const p=collect();if(!stage||Q.hash(p)!==stage.prepHash||Q.sha256($('prepResponse').value)!==stage.rawHash)throw Error('入力変更後は再照合してください');return P.response(p,$('prepResponse').value,ctx(),stage.external);}
 $('prepTransfer').onclick=()=>{try{const s=freshStage(),p=collect(),k=p.dataKind;if($('manualKind').value!==k){$('manualKind').value=k;$('manualKind').dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}for(const[id,value]of [['manualStorage',ctx().storagePath],['manualPolicy',JSON.stringify(s.policy,null,2)],['manualInput',M.toJSON(s.book,ctx(),k)]]){$(id).value=value;$(id).dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));}$('manualUnsynced').checked=ctx().confirmedUnsynced;$('manualUnsynced').dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));$('showManual').click();$('manualGate').click();$('manualPreviewButton').click();notice('比較画面へ転記しました。内容を確認して確定追加してください。');}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepSaveArchive').onclick=()=>{try{const s=freshStage();save('racelab_analysis_'+s.analysis.dataKind+'.txt',P.archive(s.book,ctx(),s.analysis.dataKind));}catch(e){notice(e.message,true);}};
 $('prepView').addEventListener('input',e=>{if(['prepRequestText','prepRepairText','prepReport'].includes(e.target.id))return;invalidate();});$('prepView').addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target.matches('select,input[type=checkbox]'))invalidate();});
 const tabs=['showManual','showAbility','showEV','showHistory','showQuality','showModel'];$('showPreparation').onclick=()=>{for(const id of ['manualView','abilityView','phase1View','historyView','qualityView','modelView'])$(id).hidden=true;for(const id of tabs)$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('prepView').hidden=false;$('showPreparation').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('prepHeading').focus();};for(const id of tabs)$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{$('prepView').hidden=true;$('showPreparation').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});
 render(P.scaffold('horse',18,'REAL'));
 // 初期画面は旧V2のまま維持。先頭タブから準備へ移動する。
})();

<script id="study-ui">
'use strict';
(()=>{
 const C=RaceStudyCore,F=RaceStudyFixtures,P=RacePreparationCore,M=RaceManualCore,Q=RaceQualityCore,$=id=>document.getElementById(id),clone=x=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
 const states={REAL:{},TEST_FIXTURE:{}},activeHashes={REAL:null,TEST_FIXTURE:null};let preparation=null,fixture=null,responseStage=null,promptStage=null,comparison=null,revision=0;
 const kind=()=>$('studyKind').value,book=()=>states[kind()][activeHashes[kind()]]||null,ctx=()=>({now:new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/,'Z'),storagePath:$('studyStorage').value,confirmedUnsynced:$('studyUnsynced').checked});
 const el=(tag,text)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e;};
 function status(s,error=false){$('studyStatus').textContent=error?'処理を保留しました。登録済みの比較評価・既存V2は保持されています。':s;$('studyError').textContent=error?s:'';}
 function lockResponse(){responseStage=null;$('studyHumanReview').checked=false;$('studyTransfer').disabled=true;$('studyAddRun').disabled=true;$('studyQuality').replaceChildren();$('studyTrace').replaceChildren();}
 function lockPrompt(){promptStage=null;$('studyPrompt').value='';$('studyCopy').disabled=true;$('studySavePrompt').disabled=true;}
 function changedSource(){revision++;preparation=null;fixture=null;lockResponse();lockPrompt();$('studySourceSummary').replaceChildren();$('studyObservations').replaceChildren();}
 function decodePreparation(raw){const d=M.parseJSON(raw);let p;if(d.format===P.T)p=P.restore(raw,ctx(),kind());else if(d.format==='RACELAB_NUMERIC_FIXTURE_V1'){if(d.dataKind!=='TEST_FIXTURE'||d.preparation?.dataKind!=='TEST_FIXTURE')throw Error('独自架空数値資料はTEST_FIXTURE専用');p=P.validate(d.preparation);}else p=P.validate(d);if(p.dataKind!==kind())throw Error('資料と選択したREAL/TEST_FIXTURE区分が不一致');return p;}
 function readPreparation(){return decodePreparation($('studyPreparation').value);}
 function source(){const p=readPreparation();P.target(p,ctx(),true);return p;}
 function showSource(p){const q=P.inspect(p,ctx()),r=p.race;$('studySourceSummary').replaceChildren(el('h4',r.raceName+' / '+p.dataKind),el('p',r.count+'名 / 事前根拠 '+q.totals.evidenced+'/'+q.totals.items+'項目（根拠充足度・採点精度ではない）'),el('p','資料の参照と時刻・許諾は利用者申告。真正性を認証していません。'));for(const[label,list]of [['HOLD',q.critical],['不足',q.missing],['除外',q.excluded.map(x=>x.key+': '+x.reasons.join('、'))]]){const d=el('details');d.append(el('summary',label+' '+list.length+'件'));const ul=el('ul');ul.className='study-list';for(const s of list)ul.append(el('li',s));d.append(ul);$('studySourceSummary').append(d);}$('studyObservations').replaceChildren();for(const runner of r.runners){const d=el('details');d.dataset.studyRunner=runner.number;d.append(el('summary',runner.number+' '+runner.name+' / 安定ID '+(runner.stableId??'欠損')));for(const item of RaceUniCore.RULES[r.sport].items){d.append(el('h5',item.label));const facts=runner.facts.filter(f=>f.topic===item.id);if(!facts.length)d.append(el('p','未取得・根拠なし（補完しません）'));for(const f of facts){let content=f.text;try{content=JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(f.text),null,2);}catch{}d.append(el('pre',content),el('p',f.id+' / '+f.ref?.sourceId+' / '+f.ref?.reference+' / 確認 '+f.ref?.checkedAt));}}$('studyObservations').append(d);}if(r.lines)$('studySourceSummary').append(el('p','ライン '+r.lines.status+': '+r.lines.groups.map(g=>g.join(' → ')).join(' / ')+' / '+r.lines.note));}
 function loadFixture(sport,count){const f=F.numeric(sport,count);$('studyKind').value='TEST_FIXTURE';$('studyStorage').value='C:\\RaceLabLocal\\manual';$('studyUnsynced').checked=true;changedSource();fixture=f;preparation=clone(f.preparation);$('studyPreparation').value=JSON.stringify(preparation,null,2);$('studyResponse').value='';$('studyOrigin').value='SIMULATED';activeHashes.TEST_FIXTURE=Q.hash(preparation);showSource(preparation);renderBook();status('SYNTHETIC / TEST_FIXTUREの数値資料を表示。期待点は手作成の試験値で実AIではありません。');}
 function download(name,text,type='text/plain;charset=utf-8'){const u=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type})),a=el('a');a.href=u;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(u),1000);status('同期対象外の指定先へ保存してください: '+name);}
 function renderQuality(s){$('studyQuality').replaceChildren(el('p','形式・計算・元の事実/参照/時刻照合: PASS','good'));$('studyQuality').append(el('p','採点の意味的妥当性: HUMAN_REQUIRED。実AI再現性: NOT_TESTED。根拠率100%でも精度100%ではありません。'));const ul=el('ul');ul.className='study-list';for(const w of s.quality.releaseHold.concat(s.quality.warnings))ul.append(el('li',w));$('studyQuality').append(ul);$('studyTrace').replaceChildren();for(const race of s.analysis.races){const table=el('table'),header=el('tr');for(const text of ['仮順位','番号・名前','点数','根拠率'])header.append(el('th',text));table.append(header);for(const row of M.ranked(s.book,race)){const tr=el('tr');for(const text of [row.rank??'未評価',row.runner.number+' '+row.runner.name,row.effective.score??'未評価',row.assessment.coverage+'%'])tr.append(el('td',text));table.append(tr);}$('studyQuality').append(table);}for(const item of s.quality.items){const d=el('details');d.dataset.studyTrace=item.number+'-'+item.itemId;d.append(el('summary',item.number+'番 '+item.itemId+' / '+(item.score??'未評価')));for(const fact of item.observations)d.append(el('pre',fact.id+': '+fact.text),el('p','参照 '+fact.ref.sourceId+' / '+fact.ref.reference+' / '+fact.ref.checkedAt));d.append(el('p','評価理由: '+item.explanation),el('p','未確認: '+item.unknowns),el('p','判断: HUMAN_REQUIRED'));$('studyTrace').append(d);}}
 function refreshActions(){const good=!!responseStage&&!responseStage.result.quality.releaseHold.length;$('studyTransfer').disabled=!good||!$('studyHumanReview').checked;$('studyAddRun').disabled=!good;}
 function validateResponse(){try{const p=source(),s=C.quality(p,$('studyResponse').value,ctx());responseStage={result:s,prepHash:Q.hash(p),rawHash:Q.sha256($('studyResponse').value)};$('studyHumanReview').checked=false;renderQuality(s);refreshActions();status('機械検査はPASS。数値と採点解釈・ライン分離は人が確認してください。');}catch(e){lockResponse();status(e.message,true);}}
 function currentResponse(){const p=source();if(!responseStage||responseStage.prepHash!==Q.hash(p)||responseStage.rawHash!==Q.sha256($('studyResponse').value))throw Error('資料/応答変更後は再照合してください');const s=C.quality(p,$('studyResponse').value,ctx());if(s.quality.releaseHold.length)throw Error('明示的なライン加点を修正するまで保留');return {p,s};}
 function renderBook(){comparison=null;$('studyComparison').replaceChildren();$('studyComparisonJSON').value='';const b=book();$('studyRunCount').textContent=(b?.runs.length||0)+'評価 / '+kind()+(b?' / '+b.preparation.race.raceId:'');for(const id of ['studyLeft','studyRight']){$(id).replaceChildren();for(const run of b?.runs||[]){const o=el('option',run.label+' ['+run.origin+']');o.value=run.id;$(id).append(o);}}if(b?.runs.length>1)$('studyRight').value=b.runs[b.runs.length-1].id;$('studyCompare').disabled=!(b?.runs.length>=2);$('studySaveBook').disabled=!b;$('studySaveReport').disabled=true;}
 for(const[id,s,n]of [['studyHorse18','horse',18],['studyKeirin7','keirin',7],['studyKeirin9','keirin',9]])$(id).onclick=()=>loadFixture(s,n);
 $('studyKind').onchange=()=>{changedSource();$('studyPreparation').value='';$('studyResponse').value='';$('studyUnsynced').checked=false;$('studyOrigin').value='USER_SUPPLIED_AI';renderBook();status('区分を分離しました。他区分の資料や集計を表示しません。');};
 for(const id of ['studyPreparation','studyStorage'])$(id).addEventListener('input',changedSource);$('studyUnsynced').addEventListener('change',changedSource);$('studyResponse').addEventListener('input',()=>{revision++;lockResponse();});$('studyHumanReview').onchange=refreshActions;for(const id of ['studyFormat','studySoftLimit'])$(id).addEventListener(id==='studyFormat'?'change':'input',lockPrompt);
 $('studyInspect').onclick=()=>{try{const p=readPreparation();showSource(p);P.target(p,ctx(),true);preparation=p;activeHashes[kind()]=Q.hash(p);renderBook();status('用途・時刻・資料形式を確認しました。意味的な正しさは未認証です。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 $('studyGenerate').onclick=()=>{try{const p=source(),s=C.request(p,ctx(),$('studyFormat').value,Number($('studySoftLimit').value)),metrics=C.measurements(p,ctx());promptStage={prepHash:Q.hash(p),format:$('studyFormat').value,soft:Number($('studySoftLimit').value),textHash:Q.sha256(s.text)};$('studyPrompt').value=s.text;$('studyMeasurements').textContent='完全版 '+metrics.full.characters+'文字 / '+metrics.full.utf8Bytes+' bytes → 簡易版 '+metrics.compact.characters+'文字 / '+metrics.compact.utf8Bytes+' bytes（'+metrics.byteReductionPercent+'%削減）。復元一致: PASS';$('studyPromptWarnings').replaceChildren(...s.stats.warnings.map(w=>el('li',w)));$('studyCopy').disabled=false;$('studySavePrompt').disabled=false;status('切捨てなしで依頼を作成しました。共有元情報の復元一致を確認しています。');}catch(e){lockPrompt();status(e.message,true);}};
 function currentPrompt(){const p=source();if(!promptStage||promptStage.prepHash!==Q.hash(p)||promptStage.format!==$('studyFormat').value||promptStage.soft!==Number($('studySoftLimit').value))throw Error('条件変更後は依頼を再作成してください');const s=C.request(p,ctx(),promptStage.format,promptStage.soft);if(promptStage.textHash!==Q.sha256(s.text))throw Error('依頼文の整合性不一致');return s;}
 $('studyCopy').onclick=async()=>{try{currentPrompt();const t=$('studyPrompt');if(navigator.clipboard?.writeText){try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(t.value);status('依頼一式を1回でコピーしました。自動送信していません。');return;}catch{}}t.focus();t.select();status('依頼一式を選択。Ctrl+Cまたは長押しコピーしてください。');}catch(e){lockPrompt();status(e.message,true);}};
 $('studySavePrompt').onclick=()=>{try{const s=currentPrompt();download('racelab_B7_request_'+kind()+'_'+s.format+'.txt',s.text);}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 for(const[id,variant]of [['studyMockA',0],['studyMockB',1]])$(id).onclick=()=>{try{const p=source();if(kind()!=='TEST_FIXTURE'||!fixture||Q.hash(p)!==Q.hash(fixture.preparation))throw Error('模擬応答は未変更の独自数値TEST_FIXTURE専用。編集した資料の点数を自動補完しません');const a=F.simulated(fixture,ctx(),variant);lockResponse();$('studyResponse').value='SYNTHETIC / TEST_FIXTURE・手作成期待値による模擬応答（実AI未実行）\n```json\n'+JSON.stringify(a,null,2)+'\n```';$('studyOrigin').value='SIMULATED';$('studyRunLabel').value='模擬応答'+(variant?'B':'A');status('模擬応答を表示。番号比例の点ではなく成績ケースに対する試験判断です。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 $('studyValidate').onclick=validateResponse;
 $('studyTransfer').onclick=()=>{try{if(!$('studyHumanReview').checked)throw Error('人の採点根拠確認が必要です');const {p,s}=currentResponse();if($('manualKind').value!==p.dataKind){$('manualKind').value=p.dataKind;$('manualKind').dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}for(const[id,value]of [['manualStorage',ctx().storagePath],['manualPolicy',JSON.stringify(s.policy,null,2)],['manualInput',M.toJSON(s.book,ctx(),p.dataKind)]]){$(id).value=value;$(id).dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));}$('manualUnsynced').checked=ctx().confirmedUnsynced;$('manualUnsynced').dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));$('showManual').click();$('manualGate').click();$('manualPreviewButton').click();status('既存V2のゲート/プレビューへ転記。確認後に確定追加してください。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 $('studyAddRun').onclick=()=>{try{const {p}=currentResponse(),hash=Q.hash(p),b=states[kind()][hash]||null;const next=C.add(b,p,$('studyResponse').value,{id:'run-'+((b?.runs.length||0)+1),label:$('studyRunLabel').value,modelDeclared:$('studyModelLabel').value,analyzedAt:ctx().now,origin:$('studyOrigin').value},ctx());states[kind()][hash]=next;activeHashes[kind()]=hash;renderBook();status('原評価を独立保存しました。JSON形式の合格は採点精度の認証ではありません。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 $('studyCompare').onclick=()=>{try{const d=C.compare(book(),$('studyLeft').value,$('studyRight').value,ctx(),kind());comparison=d;$('studyComparison').replaceChildren(el('p',d.notice+' 実AI再現性: '+d.actualAIReproducibility),el('p','USER修正 '+d.userEditsExcluded+'件は原評価の比較値から除外'));const table=el('table'),tr=el('tr');for(const h of ['番号・名前','総合点差','順位','項目/根拠差'])tr.append(el('th',h));table.append(tr);for(const r of d.rows){const tr=el('tr'),cells=[r.number+' '+r.name,(r.leftTotal??'null')+' → '+(r.rightTotal??'null')+' / 差 '+(r.totalDelta??'未評価'),(r.leftRank??'—')+' → '+(r.rightRank??'—'),r.items.filter(i=>i.delta!==0||i.nullTransition||i.addedEvidence.length||i.removedEvidence.length||i.reasonChanged).length+'項目変化'];for(const v of cells)tr.append(el('td',v));table.append(tr);const details=el('details');details.dataset.studyCompare=r.number;details.append(el('summary',r.number+'番の項目差・根拠・null・所見'));for(const i of r.items)details.append(el('p',i.id+': '+(i.left??'null')+' → '+(i.right??'null')+' / 差 '+(i.delta??'未評価')+' / null変化 '+i.nullTransition+' / 根拠追加 '+i.addedEvidence.join(',')+' / 欠落 '+i.removedEvidence.join(',')+' / 判断 '+i.contradictionReview),el('p','元理由: '+i.leftReason),el('p','先理由: '+i.rightReason));details.append(el('p','ライン/展開所見変化: '+r.lineCommentChanged+'\n'+r.leftContext+'\n→ '+r.rightContext));$('studyComparison').append(details);}$('studyComparison').prepend(table);$('studyComparisonJSON').value=JSON.stringify(d,null,2);$('studySaveReport').disabled=false;status('同じ資料の原評価差を表示。数値差は精度・的中率の証拠ではありません。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 for(const id of ['studyLeft','studyRight'])$(id).onchange=()=>{comparison=null;$('studyComparison').replaceChildren();$('studyComparisonJSON').value='';$('studySaveReport').disabled=true;};
 $('studySaveBook').onclick=()=>{try{download('racelab_B7_comparison_'+kind()+'.json',C.toJSON(book(),ctx(),kind()),'application/json;charset=utf-8');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 $('studySaveReport').onclick=()=>{try{if(!comparison)throw Error('比較を実行してください');C.validateBook(book(),ctx(),kind());download('racelab_B7_comparison_'+kind()+'.txt','能力評価の差（正確性の証拠ではない）\n'+JSON.stringify(comparison,null,2));}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};
 async function readFile(e,action){const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;const token=++revision;try{if(f.size>M.MAX)throw Error('2MiB以内です');const text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer());if(token!==revision)return;action(text);}catch(err){status(err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}}
 $('studyPreparationFile').onchange=e=>readFile(e,text=>{const p=decodePreparation(text);P.target(p,ctx(),true);changedSource();$('studyPreparation').value=JSON.stringify(p,null,2);preparation=p;activeHashes[kind()]=Q.hash(p);showSource(p);renderBook();status('資料を読込みました。元ファイルは変更しません。');});
 $('studyResponseFile').onchange=e=>{lockResponse();return readFile(e,text=>{$('studyResponse').value=text;$('studyOrigin').value='USER_SUPPLIED_AI';validateResponse();});};
 $('studyRestoreBook').onchange=e=>readFile(e,text=>{const b=C.restore(text,ctx(),kind());states[kind()][b.preparationHash]=b;activeHashes[kind()]=b.preparationHash;changedSource();$('studyPreparation').value=JSON.stringify(b.preparation,null,2);preparation=clone(b.preparation);$('studyResponse').value='';showSource(preparation);renderBook();status('資料・比較評価・ハッシュ・現在の用途条件を検査して復元しました。');});
 const tabs=['showPreparation','showManual','showAbility','showEV','showHistory','showQuality','showModel'];$('showStudy').onclick=()=>{for(const id of ['prepView','manualView','abilityView','phase1View','historyView','qualityView','modelView'])$(id).hidden=true;for(const id of tabs)$(id).setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('studyView').hidden=false;$('showStudy').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('studyHeading').focus();};for(const id of tabs)$(id).addEventListener('click',()=>{$('studyView').hidden=true;$('showStudy').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});renderBook();
})();
