<script id="identity-ui">
'use strict';
(()=>{
 const I=RaceIdentityCore,S=RaceIdentitySession,F=RaceIdentityFixtures,B=RaceIdentityBase,M=RaceManualCore,P=RacePreparationCore,C=RaceStudyCore,Q=RaceQualityCore,H=RaceHistoryCore,$=id=>document.getElementById(id),clone=x=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
 const states={REAL:null,TEST_FIXTURE:null,SYNTHETIC:null};let fixture=null,stage=null,prompt=null,revision=0,lastError='';
 const kind=()=>$('idKind').value,w=()=>states[kind()],ctx=()=>({now:new Date().toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/,'Z'),storagePath:$('idStorage').value,confirmedUnsynced:$('idUnsynced').checked});
 const el=(tag,text)=>{const e=document.createElement(tag);if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=text;return e;};
 function status(s,error=false){$('idStatus').textContent=error?'HOLD：現在の登録・固定記録は保持しています。':s;$('idError').textContent=error?s:'';if(error)lastError=s;}
 function blank(identity,preparation=null){return {format:I.WF,dataKind:identity.dataKind,identity,preparation,book:null,snapshots:[],results:[],retiredBooks:[]};}
 function invalidate(){stage=null;prompt=null;revision++;$('idCommit').disabled=true;$('idCopy').disabled=true;$('idRequest').value='';$('idAnalysisPreview').replaceChildren();}
 function preparation(){const v=M.parseJSON($('idPreparation').value);return v.format===B.P.T?B.P.restore($('idPreparation').value,ctx(),kind()):B.P.validate(v);}
 function check(external=$('idMethod').value==='EXTERNAL_AI'){if(!w())throw Error('独立名簿と候補で照合を開始してください');const p=preparation();I.preparation(w().identity,p,ctx(),external);S.requireRace(p.race,kind());return p;}
 function retainBook(reason){if(w()?.book){w().retiredBooks.push({identity:clone(w().identity),preparation:clone(w().preparation||preparation()),book:clone(w().book),invalidatedAt:ctx().now,reason});w().book=null;}}
 function showIdentity(){const state=w()?.identity;$('idRoster').replaceChildren();$('idIssues').replaceChildren();$('idLines').replaceChildren();$('idHistory').textContent='';$('idRevision').textContent='';if(!state){buttons(false);renderBook();renderSnapshots();return;}const q=I.inspect(state,ctx().now);$('idRevision').textContent='版 '+state.revision+' / 全員照合 '+q.reviewed+'/'+q.total+' / '+(q.ready?'行照合完了（真正性認証なし）':'採点・依頼・確定はHOLD');for(const row of q.rows){const box=el('article');box.className='identity-row'+(row.differences.length?' bad':'');box.dataset.identityNumber=row.number;box.append(el('strong',row.number+'番'));const pair=el('div');pair.className='name-pair';pair.append(el('p','照合元：'+(row.reference?.name??'欠員')),el('p','取込候補：'+(row.candidate?.name??'欠員')));const frame=el('p','枠：元 '+(row.reference?.frame??'未取得')+' / 候補 '+(row.candidate?.frame??'未取得')+'（主キーではない）');frame.className='frame';pair.append(frame,el('p',row.differences.join('、')||(row.reviewed?'この行を資料で確認済み':'資料の当該行で番号と氏名を確認してください')));box.append(pair);const b=el('button',row.reviewed?'行確認済み':'資料でこの行を確認');b.dataset.reviewIdentity=row.number;b.disabled=!!row.differences.length||row.reviewed;b.onclick=()=>{try{w().identity=I.review(w().identity,row.number,$('idReviewer').value,state.reference.source.reference+' / '+row.number+'番行',ctx().now);S.publish(w().identity);invalidate();showIdentity();status('この行の番号と氏名を確認した申告を保存しました。');}catch(e){status(e.message,true);}};box.append(b);$('idRoster').append(box);}const ul=el('ul');for(const e of q.errors)ul.append(el('li',(e.number===null?'全体':e.number+'番')+': '+e.message));for(const msg of q.warnings)ul.append(el('li',msg));$('idIssues').append(ul);if(state.candidate.sport==='keirin'){try{const parsed=I.parseLines(state.candidate.lines.notation,state.candidate.entries.map(e=>e.number),state.candidate.lines.status);$('idLines').append(el('h4','ライン '+state.candidate.lines.status+'（個人能力とは別）'),el('p',parsed.groups.map(g=>g.join(' → ')).join(' / ')||'構成不明'));for(const pos of parsed.positions){const e=state.candidate.entries.find(e=>e.number===pos.number);$('idLines').append(el('p',pos.number+'番 '+e.name+'：'+({SOLO:'単騎',LEADER:'先頭',SECOND:'番手',FOLLOWER:pos.position+'番手',UNKNOWN:'位置不明'}[pos.role])));if(pos.role==='SOLO')$('idLines').append(el('p','定性的シナリオ：前で好位を確保できる場合／後方や包まれる場合。実現は未確認、基礎能力へ加減算しません。'));}}catch(e){$('idLines').append(el('p','HOLD: '+e.message));}}$('idHistory').textContent=JSON.stringify({history:state.history,reviews:state.reviews},null,2);$('idCorrect').disabled=false;$('idCorrectFixture').disabled=!fixture;buttons(q.ready);renderBook();renderSnapshots();}
 function buttons(ready){for(const id of ['idGenerate','idLocal','idMock','idPreview'])$(id).disabled=!ready;$('idMock').disabled=!ready||!fixture;for(const id of ['idSaveIntakeJSON','idSaveIntakeCSV','idSaveIntakeTXT','idSaveWorkspace'])$(id).disabled=!w();}
 function renderBook(){const b=w()?.book;$('idRanking').replaceChildren();$('idEditNumber').replaceChildren();for(const id of ['idEdit','idV2JSON','idV2CSV','idV2TXT'])$(id).disabled=!b;$('idFreeze').disabled=!b;if(!b)return;const r=b.analysis.races[0],table=el('table'),tr=el('tr');for(const h of ['仮順位','番号・名前','総合点','根拠率'])tr.append(el('th',h));table.append(tr);for(const row of M.ranked(b,r)){const tr=el('tr');for(const v of [row.rank??'未評価',row.runner.number+'番 '+row.runner.name,row.effective.score??'未評価',row.assessment.coverage+'%'])tr.append(el('td',v));table.append(tr);const opt=el('option',row.runner.number+'番 '+row.runner.name);opt.value=row.runner.number;$('idEditNumber').append(opt);const d=el('details');d.append(el('summary',row.runner.number+'番の根拠・評価・修正履歴'));for(const i of row.assessment.items){d.append(el('p',i.label+': '+(i.score??'未評価')+' / '+i.reason+' / 未確認 '+i.unknowns));for(const id of i.factIds){const f=row.runner.facts.find(f=>f.id===id);d.append(el('pre',f.text),el('p',f.ref.sourceId+' / '+f.ref.reference+' / '+f.ref.checkedAt));}}d.append(el('p','USER適用: '+JSON.stringify(row.effective)),el('pre',JSON.stringify(b.edits.filter(e=>e.number===row.runner.number),null,2)));$('idRanking').append(d);}$('idRanking').prepend(table);}
 function renderSnapshots(){const selected=$('idSnapshot').value;$('idSnapshot').replaceChildren();for(const [i,snap]of (w()?.snapshots||[]).entries()){const o=el('option',(i+1)+' / '+snap.identity.candidate.raceId+' / '+snap.temporalStatus+' / '+snap.contentHash.slice(0,10));o.value=snap.contentHash;$('idSnapshot').append(o);}if(w()?.snapshots.some(s=>s.contentHash===selected))$('idSnapshot').value=selected;else if(w()?.snapshots.length)$('idSnapshot').value=w().snapshots.at(-1).contentHash;showSnapshot();$('idResults').replaceChildren();for(const r of w()?.results||[]){const d=el('details');d.open=true;d.append(el('summary','結果 '+r.registeredAt+' / '+r.evaluationStatus),el('p','関連する事前SHA: '+r.priorSnapshotHash),el('p','出典: '+r.result.source.reference+' / '+r.result.source.checkedAt),el('p','利用者の仮説: '+r.result.classification+' / '+r.result.comment));for(const row of r.result.rankings)d.append(el('p',row.place+'着 '+row.number+'番 '+row.name));for(const diff of r.differences)d.append(el('p','不一致 '+diff.number+'番: 事前 '+diff.priorName+' / 結果 '+diff.resultName));$('idResults').append(d);}}
 function showSnapshot(){const snap=w()?.snapshots.find(s=>s.contentHash===$('idSnapshot').value);$('idSnapshotInfo').textContent=snap?'レース: '+snap.identity.candidate.raceId+' / '+snap.identity.candidate.date+' / '+snap.identity.candidate.venue+' / '+snap.identity.candidate.raceNo+'R\nSHA256: '+snap.contentHash+'\n固定日時: '+snap.frozenAt+'\n'+snap.temporalStatus+'\n'+snap.notice+'\n現在の照合版: '+w().identity.revision+' / 固定時: '+snap.identity.revision:'';$('idAddResult').disabled=!snap;}
 function save(name,text,type='text/plain;charset=utf-8'){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type})),a=el('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);status('非同期の指定先へ保存してください: '+name);}
 function localPermission(){const p=preparation();if(p.dataKind!==kind())throw Error('選択区分と根拠資料が不一致');const g=B.M.gate(p.policy,ctx());if(!g.allowed)throw Error('保存HOLD: '+g.reasons.join(' / '));if(w())I.sourcePermission(w().identity,p.policy,ctx().now);return p;}
 function stageAnalysis(text){const p=check(),external=$('idMethod').value==='EXTERNAL_AI',target=B.P.target(p,ctx(),external),result=external?C.quality(p,text,ctx()):P.response(p,text,ctx(),false);if(result.quality?.releaseHold.length)throw Error('ライン加点がある評価は保留');const next=M.preview(null,target.policy,B.M.toJSON(result.book,ctx(),kind()),ctx(),kind());stage={stage:next,identityHash:Q.hash(w().identity),prepHash:Q.hash(p),inputHash:Q.sha256(text),policy:target.policy,book:result.book};$('idAnalysisPreview').replaceChildren(el('h4','プレビュー（未確定）'));for(const row of M.ranked(result.book,result.analysis.races[0]))$('idAnalysisPreview').append(el('p',row.runner.number+'番 '+row.runner.name+' / '+(row.effective.score??'未評価')+' / 根拠率'+row.assessment.coverage+'%'));$('idCommit').disabled=!!w().book;status(w().book?'同じ版に登録済みです。訂正は理由付きで別版にしてください。':'全員照合・形式・根拠・計算を検査。意味的妥当性は人が確認してください。');}
 function loadFixture(sport,count,swapped=false){const f=F.fixture(sport,count,swapped);fixture=f;$('idKind').value='TEST_FIXTURE';states.TEST_FIXTURE=blank(f.identity,clone(f.preparation));$('idReference').value=JSON.stringify(f.reference,null,2);$('idCandidate').value=JSON.stringify(f.candidate,null,2);$('idPreparation').value=JSON.stringify(f.preparation,null,2);$('idReviewer').value='TEST_USER';$('idStorage').value='C:\\RaceLabLocal\\manual';$('idUnsynced').checked=true;$('idResponse').value='';$('idResultRights').value=JSON.stringify(f.preparation.policy,null,2);$('idResultSourceId').value='fixture-cc0';invalidate();S.publish(f.identity);showIdentity();status(swapped?'独自架空の④⑦交換を再現。不一致のため依頼・採点・確定はHOLD。':'独自架空資料を表示。全員を原資料の行で照合してください。');}

 async function file(e,fn){const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;const token=++revision;try{if(f.size>I.MAX)throw Error('2MiB以内です');const text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(await f.arrayBuffer());if(token!==revision)return;fn(text);}catch(err){status(err.message,true);}finally{e.target.value='';}}
 $('idPreparationFile').onchange=e=>file(e,text=>{const d=M.parseJSON(text),p=d.format===B.P.T?B.P.restore(text,ctx(),kind()):B.P.validate(d);if(p.dataKind!==kind())throw Error('根拠区分不一致');if(w())retainBook('根拠資料の再読込により旧採点を失効');$('idPreparation').value=JSON.stringify(p,null,2);invalidate();renderBook();status('根拠資料を表示。全員照合と権利/時点を再検査してください。');});

<script id="poc-sim-core">
globalThis.RacePocCore=(()=>{
function hashSeed(str){let h=2166136261>>>0;for(let i=0;i<str.length;i++){h^=str.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619);}return h>>>0;}
function seededRandom(seedText){let s=hashSeed(String(seedText));return function(){s=(s+0x6D2B79F5)>>>0;let t=s;t=Math.imul(t^(t>>>15),t|1);t^=t+Math.imul(t^(t>>>7),t|61);return ((t^(t>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;};}
function gaussian(rng){const u=1-rng(),v=rng();return Math.sqrt(-2*Math.log(u))*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*v);}
function validateEntrants(entries){if(!Array.isArray(entries)||entries.length<3||entries.length>18)throw Error('出走数は3〜18です');return entries.map((e,i)=>{const rating=Number(e.rating),sigma=Number(e.sigma);if(!e.name||!Number.isFinite(rating)||rating<0||rating>100||!Number.isFinite(sigma)||sigma<0.1||sigma>50)throw Error((i+1)+'番の名前・能力点・ぶれを確認してください');let odds=null;if(e.odds!==''&&e.odds!==null&&e.odds!==undefined){odds=Number(e.odds);if(!Number.isFinite(odds)||odds<=1||odds>100000)throw Error((i+1)+'番の単勝オッズは1より大きい数値にしてください');}return {name:String(e.name).trim(),rating,sigma,odds};});}
function sampleFinish(entries,rng){return entries.map((e,i)=>({i,score:e.rating+e.sigma*gaussian(rng)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||a.i-b.i).map(x=>x.i);}
function runMonteCarlo(rawEntries,runs,seed){const entries=validateEntrants(rawEntries);if(!Number.isInteger(runs)||runs<1||runs>100000)throw Error('試行回数の指定が不正です');const rng=seededRandom(seed);const n=entries.length;const win=new Array(n).fill(0),top2=new Array(n).fill(0),top3=new Array(n).fill(0),rankTotal=new Array(n).fill(0);const combos=new Map();for(let k=0;k<runs;k++){const order=sampleFinish(entries,rng);for(let pos=0;pos<n;pos++){const i=order[pos];rankTotal[i]+=pos+1;if(pos===0)win[i]++;if(pos<2)top2[i]++;if(pos<3)top3[i]++;}const key=order.slice(0,3).join('-');combos.set(key,(combos.get(key)||0)+1);}const stats=entries.map((e,i)=>({id:i+1,name:e.name,rating:e.rating,sigma:e.sigma,odds:e.odds,win:win[i]/runs,top2:top2[i]/runs,top3:top3[i]/runs,avgRank:rankTotal[i]/runs,ev:e.odds===null?null:(win[i]/runs)*e.odds-1}));const seq=[...combos.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]||a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).slice(0,12).map(([key,count])=>({key,ids:key.split('-').map(i=>Number(i)+1),prob:count/runs,count}));return {runs,seed,stats,combos:seq,sumWin:win.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/runs,sumTop3:top3.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/runs};}
;return Object.freeze({seededRandom,gaussian,sampleFinish,runMonteCarlo});})();
</script>
<script id="sha256-bytes">
// Binary adapter of the unchanged Phase3A/3B8 SHA-256 implementation.
globalThis.RaceSHA256Bytes=function(s){
    const bytes=s,length=bytes.length,data=new Uint8Array(Math.ceil((length+9)/64)*64);data.set(bytes);data[length]=128;
    const view=new DataView(data.buffer);view.setUint32(data.length-8,Math.floor(length/0x20000000));view.setUint32(data.length-4,(length*8)>>>0);
    const k=[0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2];
    const h=[0x6a09e667,0xbb67ae85,0x3c6ef372,0xa54ff53a,0x510e527f,0x9b05688c,0x1f83d9ab,0x5be0cd19],w=new Uint32Array(64),rot=(x,n)=>(x>>>n)|(x<<(32-n));
    for(let offset=0;offset<data.length;offset+=64){for(let i=0;i<16;i++)w[i]=view.getUint32(offset+i*4);for(let i=16;i<64;i++){const a=w[i-15],b=w[i-2];w[i]=(w[i-16]+(rot(a,7)^rot(a,18)^(a>>>3))+w[i-7]+(rot(b,17)^rot(b,19)^(b>>>10)))>>>0;}
      let [a,b,c,d,e,f,g,j]=h;for(let i=0;i<64;i++){const t1=(j+(rot(e,6)^rot(e,11)^rot(e,25))+((e&f)^(~e&g))+k[i]+w[i])>>>0,t2=((rot(a,2)^rot(a,13)^rot(a,22))+((a&b)^(a&c)^(b&c)))>>>0;j=g;g=f;f=e;e=(d+t1)>>>0;d=c;c=b;b=a;a=(t1+t2)>>>0;}const v=[a,b,c,d,e,f,g,j];for(let i=0;i<8;i++)h[i]=(h[i]+v[i])>>>0;
    }return h.map(n=>n.toString(16).padStart(8,'0')).join('');
  };

</script>
<script id="import-core">
'use strict';
globalThis.RaceImportCore=(()=>{
 const I=RaceIdentityCore,Q=RaceQualityCore,H=RaceHistoryCore,M=globalThis.RaceIdentityBase?.M||RaceManualCore;
 const FORMAT='RACELAB_IMPORT_PACK_V1',RUN='RACELAB_SIMULATION_RUN_V1',MAX=2*1024*1024;
 const clone=x=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)),fail=x=>{throw Error(x);};
 const object=(o,keys,label)=>{if(!o||Array.isArray(o)||typeof o!=='object'||Object.keys(o).sort().join('|')!==[...keys].sort().join('|'))fail(label+'の形式/フィールド不正');};
 const text=(s,label,max=2000)=>{if(typeof s!=='string'||s.length>max)fail(label+'は文字列です');};
 const limited=s=>{if(typeof s!=='string'||Q.utf8(s).length>MAX)fail('入力はUTF-8 2MiB以内です');return s;};
 function validate(input){limited(JSON.stringify(input));const p=clone(input);object(p,['format','dataKind','policy','races'],'一括資料');if(p.format!==FORMAT||!['REAL','SYNTHETIC','TEST_FIXTURE'].includes(p.dataKind))fail('一括資料の形式/区分が不正');if(p.policy!==null){p.policy=M.policyValidate(p.policy);if(p.policy.dataKind!==p.dataKind)fail('許諾の区分不一致');}if(!Array.isArray(p.races)||p.races.length<1||p.races.length>50)fail('レースは1〜50件です');const seen=new Set();
  for(const r of p.races){if(r.features===undefined)r.features=null;object(r,['card','ratings','conditions','identity','v2','features',...(r.pdfEvidence!==undefined?['pdfEvidence']:[]),...(r.lineEvidence!==undefined?['lineEvidence']:[])],'レース');r.card=I.card(r.card);if(r.lineEvidence!==undefined){object(r.lineEvidence,['format','reference','reviewer','reviewedAt','identityHash','lineHash','referenceHash','v2Hash','ratingsHash','v2BaseReviewed'],'ライン確認');r.lineEvidence.reference=I.card(r.lineEvidence.reference);}if(r.pdfEvidence!==undefined)RaceKeirinPDF.validateEvidence(r.pdfEvidence,r.card,p.dataKind);const key=r.card.sport+'|'+r.card.raceId;if(seen.has(key))fail('重複レース: '+key);seen.add(key);if(!Array.isArray(r.ratings)||r.ratings.length!==r.card.entries.length)fail('能力入力の人数不一致');const ns=new Set();for(const e of r.ratings){object(e,['number','score','sigma','style','notes'],'能力入力');if(ns.has(e.number)||!r.card.entries.some(x=>x.number===e.number))fail('能力入力の番号重複/未知');ns.add(e.number);if(e.score!==null&&(!Number.isFinite(e.score)||e.score<0||e.score>100))fail('能力点は0〜100かnull');if(e.sigma!==null&&(!Number.isFinite(e.sigma)||e.sigma<0.1||e.sigma>50))fail('ぶれは0.1〜50かnull');text(e.style,'脚質',300);text(e.notes,'根拠',10000);}if(!r.conditions||typeof r.conditions!=='object'||Array.isArray(r.conditions))fail('競技条件はobjectです');for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(r.conditions)){text(k,'条件名',100);if(v!==null&&!['string','number','boolean'].includes(typeof v))fail('条件は値またはnullです');if(typeof v==='number'&&!Number.isFinite(v))fail('条件数値が不正');}
   if(r.identity!==null){r.identity=I.validate(r.identity);if(r.identity.dataKind!==p.dataKind)fail('照合の区分不一致');}
   if(r.features!==null&&(!Array.isArray(r.features)||r.features.length>18))fail('特徴量は出走者別の配列かnullです');if(r.v2!==null){if(!p.policy)fail('V2には既存用途台帳が必要です');r.v2=M.bookValidate(r.v2,{now:r.card.source.checkedAt,storagePath:p.policy.sources[0]?.allowedLocalRoot+'\\import',confirmedUnsynced:true},p.dataKind);if(r.v2.analysis.races.length!==1)fail('V2はレース別に保持してください');const a=r.v2.analysis.races[0];for(const key of ['sport','raceId','date','venue','raceNo','count'])if(a[key]!==r.card[key])fail('V2と出走表の'+key+'不一致');for(const e of r.card.entries)if(!a.runners.some(x=>x.number===e.number&&x.name===e.name&&x.stableId===e.stableId))fail('V2の番号/名前/安定ID不一致');}
  }return p;
 }
 function lineVerified(r,p,ctx){const e=r.lineEvidence;if(!e)return false;const ref=e.reference,card=r.card;if(e.format!=='RACELAB_LINE_CONFIRMATION_V1'||typeof e.reviewer!=='string'||!e.reviewer.trim()||e.v2BaseReviewed!==true)fail('ラインの独立資料・V2基礎点の確認が不足');if(!r.identity||e.identityHash!==Q.hash(r.identity)||e.lineHash!==Q.hash(card.lines)||e.referenceHash!==Q.hash(ref)||e.v2Hash!==Q.hash(r.v2)||e.ratingsHash!==Q.hash(r.ratings))fail('ライン確認後に入力・照合・V2が変更されています');if(ref.source.kind!=='USER_CONFIRMED_LIST'||!ref.source.independentFromCandidate||ref.source.sourceId===card.source.sourceId||ref.source.reference===card.source.reference)fail('候補と同じ出所は独立したライン資料にできません');for(const k of ['sport','raceId','date','venue','raceNo','startsAt','count'])if(ref[k]!==card[k])fail('独立ライン資料のレース条件不一致');const entries=x=>x.entries.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.number-b.number);if(Q.hash(entries(ref))!==Q.hash(entries(card)))fail('独立ライン資料の番号・氏名・出走者不一致');const a=I.parseLines(ref.lines.notation,ref.entries.map(x=>x.number),ref.lines.status),b=I.parseLines(card.lines.notation,card.entries.map(x=>x.number),card.lines.status);if(!a.groups.length||Q.hash(a.groups)!==Q.hash(b.groups)||ref.lines.status!==card.lines.status)fail('独立ライン資料の構成・状態が不一致');const reviewed=Date.parse(e.reviewedAt),checked=Date.parse(ref.source.checkedAt),available=Date.parse(ref.source.availableAt),now=Date.parse(ctx.now),start=Date.parse(card.startsAt);if(!/^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\d(?:Z|[+-]\d\d:\d\d)$/.test(e.reviewedAt)||![reviewed,checked,available,now,start].every(Number.isFinite)||new Date(e.reviewedAt.slice(0,10)+'T00:00:00Z').toISOString().slice(0,10)!==e.reviewedAt.slice(0,10)||Number(e.reviewedAt.slice(11,13))>23||Number(e.reviewedAt.slice(14,16))>59||Number(e.reviewedAt.slice(17,19))>59||available>checked||checked>reviewed||reviewed>now||reviewed>=start)fail('ライン資料の事前利用時刻が未確認/矛盾');if(!p.policy)fail('ライン資料の利用条件が未確認');const src=p.policy.sources.find(x=>x.id===ref.source.sourceId);if(!src||src.permissions.localAnalysis.status!=='CONFIRMED'||src.permissions.localStorage.status!=='CONFIRMED')fail('ライン資料の利用条件が未確認');const gate=M.gate({...p.policy,analysisMethod:'USER_ONLY'},ctx);if(!gate.allowed)fail(gate.reasons.join(' / '));return true;}
